Reached this way: `flow_1082d0_from_column`, `flow_side_char_random` and `game_hook_27134`'s target were all already ported and, for the latter two, already documented by name in their own header comments as this task's exact call sites, before this task wired the calls that make those citations real. New `check_mode_22`/`check_mode_23`/`check_mode_24` in `test_fight.c` (registered right after `check_mode_1f`, same free-list-accumulation placement reason): the shared field copy and epilogue flags for 0x22/0x24 (via `tf_demo_fixture`'s own latch sentinels, already proven distinguishing by `check_arena_frame`); a **differential** rng-state check for the `DS_00104AC4 > 1` dust gate (an absolute "untouched" check is unsafe since other real callees in the same function draw rng too, just identically across the compared runs); the unconditional prefix of `flow_26d4c`'s gated body, using the actor-spawn arm to avoid any `ENGLISH.TXT` dependency; all four of mode 0x23's states in isolation with a fresh scratch pool each (`m23_seed`); and mode 0x24's countdown-expired vs not-expired paths, with `DS_00104B02`'s seven gate bytes explicitly cleared so `flow_side_char_random`'s draw loop cannot spin. Building these tests broke a pre-existing one: `check_mode_tail`'s `H` sub-case used mode 0x22/0x23 specifically *because* they were inert gaps at the time, to isolate `game_frame`'s second (mode-tail) switch's own already-ported `case 0x22/0x23` block; once real, `flow_26d4c`'s `DS_00104AC4 <= 0` arm fired under the old fixture (leftover `DS_00104AC4`) and reassigned the running mode to `0x24` before that second switch read it, and mode 0x23's own state machine could fire on whatever `DS_00104B25` happened to survive from an earlier test — fixed by adding `DS_00104AC4 = 1` and `DS_00104B25 = 0` to the shared `MT_SEED` fixture macro (inert for every other mode number it drives). One test-authoring bug (not a port bug) was caught by the test itself on first run and fixed: mode 0x23 state 3's `DS_00104ABC` formula's two expected values were transposed between its two branches. Four mutations (mode 0x22's dust gate threshold, mode 0x23 state 1's `DS_00104AC4` constant, mode 0x24's countdown-expiry threshold) each fail the suite (the last one, 9 assertions in one run) and were reverted. `PR_ORACLE_REQUIRED=1 run_tests` is green three times (plus the 4 mutation-revert runs), 0 compiler warnings, ≈2s each, no SIGBUS, no hang. `make verify` is unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained at N = 3617, `symbols.h` idempotent) — expected, since the no-input demo/attract/front-end paths never store any of these three modes. Remaining named gaps: the standing `0x32970` run-clock and `0x2C3FC` voice `PORT:` no-ops (spec §7, record §45-A) in `flow_26d4c` and mode 0x23's state 2; a second, unported `0x1DA84` call site (`0x1DC5C`, outside this task's three targets, noted not chased).

**Mode `0x25`'s inline case (`0x266AC`/`0x4EF8C`/`0x4F0FC`, record §49-P).** `game_frame`'s case `0x25` is genuinely inline (`get_xrefs_to 0x266AC` returns exactly one caller, `0x252B2` inside `FUN_00024c5c` itself, unlike the handler-function modes this session already ported): the `DS_00104B25` sub-state dispatch now runs as a real `case 0x25u:` block, calling three new functions — `game_mode_25_step` (`0x266AC`), `game_mode_25_reveal` (`0x4EF8C`) and `game_mode_25_exit` (`0x4F0FC`), `flow.c` — plus the already-ported `fight_effects_pass` (`0x49C78`). Porting `0x266AC` faithfully pulled in its full, previously-unported callee closure: the mode's per-frame audience-effects pass `0x4E67C` (`fight_4e67c`, `fight.c`) and its four callees (two arrival tests `0x4A808`/`0x4A8A8`, the camera-hit-test/worshipper-spawn `0x4E5A4`, the round-counter/tally update `0x4E99C`), the round-card renderer `0x4EBB8` (`fight_mode25_scorecard`) both `0x4E67C` and `0x4EF8C` call, and the per-side "combo/approach" chain `0x266AC` calls twice a frame (`0x384F8`/`fight_384f8`, `0x382C4`/`fighter_382c4`, `0x38434`/`fighter_38434`, `0x3D004`/`hit_3d004`, all `fighter.c`) — nine new functions total, none named by the task brief's leads, each found by walking `get_xrefs_to`/`disassemble_function` outward until every callee resolved to an already-ported function or the `0x2C3FC` deferred-voice idiom (the only deferred-idiom calls in the closure, both inside `0x4E99C`). Two functions moved from `static` to exported because the new code calls them directly (not through `fn_resolve`, matching the raw's own plain `CALL`): `camera_dust_spawn` (`0x1282C`, `camera.c`) and `actor_type_49444` (`0x49444`, `actors.c`). The raw disassembly corrected two decompiler mis-readings in `0x266AC` itself (`_DAT_001077e8 = 0` decompiles where the raw is a copy from `DS_001077E4`, and five `FUN_00017fa0` calls decompile with wrong/dropped arguments where the raw shows the standard per-side `DS_00100Bxx` constants, carried dead through the preceding `camera_screen_base` calls since that callee doesn't clobber them) and one wrong first pass of this derivation's own (`0x384F8`'s wall-clamp `Jcc` pair reads opposite of "clamp before reaching the threshold" — the call actually fires once the position has already reached or passed it). New `check_mode_25` in `test_fight.c`: `game_mode_25_step`'s preamble and pulse-timer gate (both arms: closed leaves `DS_001088A6`/`DS_00104B18` untouched, open at 0 resets to `0xB4` and arms, open above 0 merely decrements) against an empty audience list and null combo nodes (`fight_reset_recs`/`fight_reset_actors`); `game_mode_25_exit`'s four pure global writes; `game_mode_25_reveal` plus `fight_mode25_scorecard` driven to two different, arithmetic-derived totals (an unequal `7`/`6` pair and an equal `5`/`5` pair) checked against `DS_0010888C`/`DS_00108891` directly; and the one-entry audience-list settling loop. The settling-loop test caught its own bug on first run (`test_fight.c:30456: 127 != 0`): checking `& 0x7F == 0` cannot distinguish "the `&= 0x7F` mask ran" from "it never did" once the sentinel's own low 7 bits are already `0x7F` — fixed to check bit `0x80` alone. Three mutations (`fight_mode25_scorecard`'s `acc = sum` to `sum + 1u`, `game_mode_25_step`'s `DS_00104B18 = 1u` to `0u`, `game_mode_25_exit`'s `| 1u` to `| 4u`) each fail the suite and were reverted. `PR_ORACLE_REQUIRED=1 run_tests` is green three times (plus the three mutation-revert runs), 0 compiler warnings, ≈1.7-1.8s each, no hang, no crash — `fight_4e67c`'s per-entry `while` and `fight_mode25_scorecard`'s two `for`s each terminate on the same sentinel-equality or fixed-count test their raw `Jcc` pair does. `make verify` is unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained at N = 3617, `symbols.h` idempotent) — expected, since mode `0x25` (the round-tally screen after a best-of-N decision) is reachable only from a live match's own round-end transition, never from the no-input demo/attract/front-end capture windows. No named gaps remain within the `0x266AC`/`0x4EF8C`/`0x4F0FC` closure; `fight_hud_pulse`'s header comment is updated to drop `0x266AC` from its "unported caller" list.

**Modes `0x28`-`0x2F`'s eight game-start entries (record §49-Q).** `game_frame`'s cases `0x28` through `0x2F` — the generic named-gap comment's own `0x28..0x2F inline (0x2D974 field 0x29, 0x2CA7C, 0x257A4)` listing — now run their own code, ported as `game_mode_28_step` through `game_mode_2f_step` (`flow.c`) and wired individually. Unlike most of this batch's siblings, all three ingredients the task brief pointed at were already fully ported before this task started: `config_field_get` (`0x2D974`), `config_credit_spend` (`0x2CA7C`) and `game_coin_divert` (`0x257A4`, record §47-C) — so this task really was mostly wiring, confirmed by reading every instruction in all eight blocks rather than assumed from the brief's own framing. The real per-case entry addresses (`0x24F09`/`0x24F66`/`0x24FC4`/`0x25187`/`0x2501E`/`0x25071`/`0x250CE`/`0x2512B`) had to come from the jump table at `0x24B8C` itself (`read_memory`, one dword per case), not disassembly position, since `0x24C5C` dispatches through a jump table. Each block is short, branch-free and loop-free: (re-)decode config field `0x29` into `DS_00104528`/`DS_00105B3A`/`DS_0010452C`/`DS_001088D0`, exactly the idiom `game_init`'s own `0x20C5D`-`0x20CC2` block already established (record §46-F) — skipped only by case `0x2B`, the one case with no `config_field_get` call at all — then call `game_coin_divert` with a per-case players mask (1/2/3 for one side, the other side, or both). Cases `0x28`/`0x29`/`0x2A`/`0x2B` also store a per-case constant into the dword `DS_00104AB8` (1/2/3/3); cases `0x2C`-`0x2F` store nothing there at all, confirmed by reading every one of their instructions, not an omission in this derivation — they leave whatever an earlier case (or `game_state_init`'s own init to 0) left in place. Cases `0x2D`-`0x2F` additionally spend one credit (`config_credit_spend(1)`) before diverting. Cases `0x2E` and `0x2F` turned out to be the compiler's own byte-for-byte duplicate of the same operation (`config_credit_spend(1)` then `game_coin_divert(2)`) emitted twice under two separate case labels — ported as two separate functions rather than merged into one, per this repo's one-function-per-jump-table-entry convention, the same choice records §49-L/§49-M/§49-N already made for a structurally similar near-duplicate. One genuine decompiler trap surfaced and was resolved by reading the raw, not trusting the decompilation: Ghidra's `port/decomp/prage.c` shows cases `0x28`/`0x29` assigning `DS_00104AB8` from `extraout_ECX_00`/a second call parameter, making `FUN_0002d974` look two-argument; the raw disassembly shows `ESI = 1` (case `0x28`) / `ECX = 2` (case `0x29`) are loaded *before* the `CALL 0x2D974` but read only *after* it returns, on a path the callee never touches — scheduling, not a parameter, and `config_field_get`'s existing single-argument signature is unchanged. New `check_modes_28_2f` in `test_fight.c` (registered right after `check_mode_21`) exercises all eight from one shared config-field-`0x29` seed, proving: the decode (and, for case `0x2B`, that it is skipped — `DS_00104528` survives a sentinel untouched); the per-case `DS_00104AB8` store or its deliberate absence (a `0x66666666` sentinel proven to survive cases `0x2C`-`0x2F` untouched); the credit spend for `0x2D`-`0x2F` (the counter decrementing by exactly 1 with the suppression gate open, plus a ninth sub-case proving the free-play early return leaves it alone); and the players argument reaching `game_coin_divert`, read back off its own `DS_00104B1F` post-condition — the same proxy the existing `0x257A4` test at `test_fight.c:6839` already uses. `DS_001014F4` (the actor record pool base) is pinned to 0 for the fixture's duration so `game_coin_divert`'s `actors_reset_al(0)` call takes its "pool not loaded" early return instead of touching this file's shared `FIGHT_RECS`/`FIGHT_ACTORS` fixtures. Proved live: mutated `game_mode_28_step`'s `DS_00104AB8` store from 1 to 2, rebuilt, confirmed `check_modes_28_2f` fails (`test_fight.c:30372: 2 != 1`), reverted, rebuilt clean. None of the eight new functions contains a loop of any kind, so this task carries none of the loop-exit-condition risk the sibling `gap26-modes7f` session's `run_tests` hang (caught and fixed separately) warned this batch to check for. `PR_ORACLE_REQUIRED=1 run_tests` is green three times (plus the mutation and its revert), ≈1.8s each, no SIGBUS, no hang. `make verify` (run with worktree-local `*_DUMP`/`TITLE_PIN_DIR` overrides, `_gap36` suffixed, to avoid the shared-`/tmp` collision precedent already flagged by records §49-H/§49-K/§49-O) is unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained at N = 3617, `symbols.h` idempotent) — expected, since modes `0x28`-`0x2F` are reachable only through an accepted coin/start event or the still-`DS_00108173`-gated state 8 arm, neither of which fires on the no-input path. No named gaps remain from this task: `config_field_get`, `config_credit_spend` and `game_coin_divert` were already gap-free, and every instruction in all eight new blocks is accounted for.

**The volleyball mini-game's per-frame driver `0x4BF18` (record §49-S).** Mode `0x21`'s `0x26540` (`game_mode_21_step`, record §49-O) called `fight_effects_pass`'s replacement `FUN_0004BF18` as a named gap since that batch landed; it is now ported in full as `fight_4bf18` (`fight.c`) and wired at `0x26687`, closing the gap. At 458 raw instructions it is considerably larger than the task brief's "~150 instruction" lead, and the brief's other lead — a "doubly-linked entry list" — did not hold either: the full disassembly shows `0x4BF18` walks the exact same singly-linked `DS_0010884C` effects list `fight_effects_pass`/`fight_effects_hold_all` already walk (head sentinel, `next` field only, no second/prev field read anywhere), just with its own 8-state switch (jump table `0x4BEF4`, read by hand with `read_memory`; types `0`, `7` and `>8` all land on the same default body) and a shared per-entry tail. That tail calls the already-ported `fight_4c60c` and, on a same-frame ground strike, scores the rally itself (`DS_0010889C[idx] += 1`, `DS_00108898 = idx + 1`, the two `actors_anim_begin` calls picking sides/streams from `fighter_actor_bit15_clear(0)` and a ball-position/midpoint compare) before an early return that abandons the rest of the entry list for the frame — a real, deliberate raw behaviour, not a derivation artefact. `DS_0010889B[DS_00108898]`, read at one point as the "reached 3" gate, folds algebraically to `DS_0010889C[idx]` (`0x10889B + (idx+1) == 0x10889C + idx`), so no name was invented for the unnamed `DS_0010889B`. `0x4A868` (case 1's else branch) is the same unported gate this codebase already treats as always false in `fight_effects_pass`'s case-13/14 bodies and `fight_effects_idle_pass` — reused verbatim, not re-derived, per the task brief's own instruction. One new callee was needed: `fight_4cd98` (`0x4CD98`, DS_00108864's third writer alongside `0x4BD98` and `fight_4c784`, already named by `fight_4ac18`'s own header) — the match-timeout end the post-loop `DS_001088A0` countdown reaches on hitting 0 (mode `<- 6`, score bytes clear, the ball entry resets, both worshipper actors die). That countdown's own `test ax,ax; ja` is confirmed unsigned, not the signed mistake this session's review notes flagged as a recurring risk; the post-loop `DS_00104AF4 % DS_001088D0` is likewise confirmed unsigned (`EDX` is pre-cleared before the `idiv`). `check_mode_21` (`test_fight.c`) is updated rather than left stale: its old canary proved `0x4BF18` was a skipped gap (`DS_00108874` stayed at its sentinel through `game_mode_21_step`); flipped to prove `fight_4bf18`'s own tail write now fires, plus a new scenario seeding `DS_001088A0 = 1` to drive the timeout path into `fight_4cd98`, checking the mode word's low half (the high half is a `k48_seed` dword sentinel proving only a partial word write happened) and the score bytes (seeded non-zero first, so the zero result is a real transition, not an unseeded-BSS read). Verified live by mutation: commenting out the `flow.c` call site made every new/changed assertion fail, then restored. `PR_ORACLE_REQUIRED=1 run_tests` is green three times, ≈1.75s each, no hang — the list walk's exit condition was diffed against `fight_effects_hold_all`'s own proven-correct walk before writing it, given this session's separate `run_tests`-hang incident on a mistranslated loop exit. `make verify` is unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained at N = 3617, `symbols.h` idempotent) — expected, since the volleyball mini-game is reachable only mid-attract-loop past the demo fight, outside every oracle's own capture window. One named gap remains, deliberately: `FUN_0004A868` (case 1's else branch), out of scope per spec §7.4 and the precedent this task reused; its one voice call site is unreachable in any case, since it sits behind that same gate.

**The engine/HUD helper cluster `0x1B000`-`0x1E500` and the `0x21994` pair (record §49-V).** The batch list came from a header-only heuristic, so each address was re-checked before porting: `0x1B120` (`res_load_index`) and `0x1CB18` (`game_sample_play`) were already ported under non-standard headers, and `0x468D8` (a callee) was already the static `ai_pred_468d8`, now exported. `0x1BBAC` (the ISR's joystick/keyboard sampler, host-owned in the port), `0x1C0F0`/`0x1E30C`/`0x1E458` (the DPMI extended-memory block list the port replaces with flat bump allocators) and `0x204F4` (the name-entry prep whose only caller `0x1EC38` is on `gap37-hiscorerank`) have no ported caller and are documented, not ported. Ported: `0x1D540` (`fight_hud_meter_step`, the render table's bit 1 handler, with `0x1D4D0` and `0x1D74C`) — it steps the two HUD meters `DS_0010290C/E` toward the slots' `+0x5A/+0x5D` targets (redrawing through the already-ported `0x1D2F0`/`0x1D464`), runs the `+0x5D/+0x5E` counters and clears `DS_00104AEC` bit 1; the port had no entry registered for that bit, so the bit stayed set forever, and it is now registered in `actors_init`. Also ported: the pair `0x21994` arms, `0x216EC` (`fighter_216ec`, the slot `+0x0C` six-state step) and `0x21458` (`fighter_21458`, the slot `+0x10` recovery step, registered through the case-10 adapter `fighter_21458_case10` because the port's case 10 passes only `(slot, side)`), their callee `0x21F30` (`0x3F308`'s body duplicated by the compiler) and the installer `0x21994` itself, whose callers are unported call sites (so it is dead in the port until they are). One raw quirk is replicated and commented: `0x216EC` state 4 indexes the `0xBD882` table by the side (`EAX` is still `side * 2`), state 2 by the character. New `check_hud_meter_step` and `check_pair_callbacks` (`test_fight.c`): 13 and 20 single-site mutations respectively fail the suite (two first-pass survivors in the former were closed with an equal-to-`lim` case and a `+0x5D == 1` case). `make verify` (worktree-local `_gap41` dumps) is unchanged — front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained at N = 3617, `symbols.h` idempotent — including the run with only `0x1D540` wired, the one change here that touches an oracle-covered path. Named gaps: the ones above, the `0x2C3FC` voice posts in `0x21F30` (spec §7), and the meaning of the `0x1D540` slot bytes.

**The high-score name-entry screen `0x1F458` and its callees (record §49-T).** `game_mode_1e_step`'s states 5/8/0xB..0xE now poll `nameentry_step` (`0x1F458`, params 0/1/0/1) and advance on its 0 (successor states 6, 9, 0xF, 0x10, 2, 2, each with the `DS_00104AFE = 0x8C`/`DS_001088EE = 0`/`DS_00104B00 = 0x15`/`DS_00104AFA = 0x1E` quad). New `port/src/game/nameentry.c` holds six ported functions: `0x1F458` itself (timer, the four-way cursor, the letter grid, the header/score/rank/blink text, letter/DEL/END selection from the face buttons or the keyboard queue), `0x1FFD0` (the 18-cell flying-letter animator, nine states, jump table `0x1FFAC`), `0x20710` (the finaliser: name filter, `0x2DCA0` inserts into tables 1 and 0, the rank line, retiring the three cursor actors), `0x13EF0`/`0x13F68` (the bad-word filter over the `0x9AF40` table, a fuzzy matcher with its own recursion and a tolerance byte) and, because the parked states 0xF/0x10 and the rank-probe states re-arm the screen through it, `0x1ED2C` (the screen reset). Findings, raw first: the queue at `0x104458` has 17 live entries (both the reader `0x1FD7F` and the writer `0x20860` index it before their `& 0xF`); the up/down autorepeat arms are unreachable (they share the right/left counters `DS_001044E0`/`DS_001044DC`, which no code writes); the letter index is `7 * ((row - 6) / 3) + (col - 0xB) / 3` and puts DEL and END at 27/28 with no special case; state 3 clamps to the *old* target. `check_name_filter`, `check_nameentry_cells`, `check_nameentry_finish`, `check_nameentry_step` and `check_mode_1e_nameentry` in `test_fight.c` seed sentinels for every field and compare drawn text against direct draws; 18 mutations (floor vs truncating divides, dropped side test, masked queue read, off-by-one comparisons, swapped wrap columns, a wrong side in the wiring, ...) each fail the suite and were reverted. `check_mode_1e_parked` now lists only states 1/0xF/0x10. Remaining named gaps: `0x1EC38`/`0x1ECC8`/`0x2DDE4` (record §49-R) and `0x204F4`, which write the geometry words this screen reads, so states 0xF/0x10 stay parked; `0x20860` (keyboard char -> queue) is unported so the queue is empty in the port; the `0x2C3FC` voices (§45-A).

**gap40-posecb (record §49-U)** ported the four fighter pose/animation slot callbacks `0x3E6A8` (`fighter_3e6a8`, the `+0x10` handler `0x3EC20` stores in the other slot; `0x3531C` case 10, `(slot, side)`, the record reloaded from the slot as `fighter_3d424` does), `0x3EA24`, `0x3EE00` and `0x3F9C8` (`+0x0C` callbacks, `0x3531C` case 7, `(slot, rec, side)`), all registered in `actors.c` and declared in `fighter.h`. They have no direct callers: the storing setups are `0x3EC20` (`0x3EA24` + `0x3E6A8`), `0x3EF44` (`0x3EE00`; the `+0x0C` dword of character 0 reactions `0x22`/`0x23` and character 5 reaction `0x21` in `0xA3528`) and `0x3FB88` (`0x3F9C8`; character 0 reactions `0x28`/`0x29`), none of which Ghidra's exported function list contains. Every callee was already ported; the `0x2C3FC(0xBB)` voice at `0x3EED3` and the two `0x62003` error exits are the deferred idioms (`PORT:` notes). Two raw quirks are kept and recorded: `0x3EA24` case 4 indexes the landing-word table `0xBD882` by *side* (EAX still holds `side*2` from `0x3EA35`), not by character, and `0x3F9C8` case 6 uses the `+0x2C` loaded at `0x3FB34` before `0x188AC` for the later `0x188DC`. `check_posecb` (registered after `check_sc_char5`) covers every arm with signed/unsigned and equality edge rows for both sides; nineteen live mutations (25/11/3/21/22/3/2/5/7/9/6/7/4/6/3/3/22/3/43 failures) each failed the suite and were reverted. `PR_ORACLE_REQUIRED=1 run_tests` green 3x (~1.8 s); `make verify` gates unchanged (the callbacks are reachable only through the unported setups). Named gaps: the setups `0x3EC20`, `0x3EF44`, `0x3FB88`, their caller `0x3E9A4` (with `0x3E800`/`0x3E8E4`/`0x3E924`), and the stored `+0x18`/`+0x1C` callbacks `0x3ED78`, `0x3EDB8`, `0x3F7F4`, `0x3F85C`.

**The startup/frame helper cluster (record §49-W, branch `gap42-startuphelpers`).** Of the six heuristic candidates, three are ported and three are a documented named gap. `0x129FC`, `0x14A5C` and `0x14B90` are ported with the callees that complete them: `0x12BB8` (the dust burst), `0x4DBB4` (a capped `+0x5B` byte add), `0x14988`, `0x14938`, `0x14950` and `0x14C98`. `camera_impact_dust_spawn` (`0x128D4`, §49-L) had returned at its two gates; the raw falls through to a tail, now ported: outside mode `0x24` it resets `DS_000F0AE8` to `0xFF` when the named slot's `+0x52` is 0 and offers the in-use dust nodes to `0x129FC` until one hits a fighter whose character/reaction pair is in that character's raw set (characters 0/3/5, 1/6, 2/4 have three different sets), which bursts the dust (`0xC976C` spawn, `0x2B150`, `+0x5B` += 1). `0x14B90` and `0x14C98` are character 3's reactions `0x26`/`0x28` (`*(u32*)0xA4720`/`0xA4748` of `0x34E2C`'s table), the twins of `0x14814`/`0x1490C` with a `0x3C520`-based start, an extra `0x3C148`/`0x3C16C`, the FD100/FD110/FD11A data and `0x14988`/`0x14A5C` as the step machine; they are registered. `0x102B8`, `0x1013C` and `0x10A68` are the Smacker SDK's audio-streaming layer (`0x10000..0x10D70`, callers all in the SDK `0x62xxx..0x6Axxx` or the unported `0x100DC`): they hold AIL sample handles the port keeps as host pointers, `0x102B8`'s body is unreachable through the inert `AIL_stream_buffer_index` stub, and `0x10A68` is file I/O; they stay unported (evidence in §49-W.2). New `check_char3_2628` and `check_dust_consume` in `test_fight.c`; the mutation runs are recorded in §49-W.5. No oracle is expected to move: mode `0x22` (the tail's only caller) is not on the front-end, demo-fight or attract2 paths.

**The `0x2E000`-`0x32000` config/key/menu helper cluster (record §49-Y).** Nine functions are ported into `config.c` (tests in `test_game.c`, `test_cfg_helpers`): the timed screen `0x2EA78` (`config_screen_wait`: one frame built and presented without the game logic, then `n + 2` ticks of key-queue draining into the latch `DS_00105F30`; the ISR is modelled as one tick per retrace), its key layer `0x2EB80` (idle check), `0x2EBF0` (latched key to the arrow/Enter/Esc bit word through the key-config record at `DS_00101514`), `0x2EDE0` and `0x2EEC8` (its only callers), and the menu drawers `0x305FC` (code-entry row, files a name and number in high-score table 2), `0x30788` (a 32-cell bar plus a clamped number), `0x31E28` (an option row with a value string and `<`/`>` arrows) and `0x3157C` (key word to key name, with the `<%s>` wrap). None has a ported caller (the callers are unported regions and the sibling menu cluster), and `0x2EA78` is deliberately not wired into `config_set_defaults` (it would present frames and wait 180 ticks inside `game_init`). `0x2D638` and `0x2DE98` are intentionally deferred: they are the storage load layer the eeprom-config design (spec §7) declared a no-op, not helpers. Named gaps: the storage family (`0x2E990`, `0x2D4EC`, `0x2D498`, `0x2DF8C`), the ISR's `0x1BBAC`/`0x2D62C`, `0x5004A` and the `0x65431` idle longjmp.

**The `0x45000`-`0x4F500` fighter/AI/effects helper cluster (record §49-Z).** Ten functions: `0x45B50`, `0x47A00`, `0x47B04`, `0x496DC`, `0x4A928`, `0x4CF20`, `0x4F4E8` from the list, plus the two callees they needed, `0x39FB0` and `0x4AA6C`, and `0x474E4`, which was already ported as `string_decode` under a `/* PORT: */` header the progress counter does not match (header fixed, body checked against the raw). `0x4CF20` is wired: `fight_dust_build`'s `DS_00104AFA == 0x23` arm (now read as the word the raw reads) calls it, and the record §10.5 claim that it uses "no list, no RNG" is corrected — it moves six nodes and draws 18 times. `0x4F4E8` (`flow_round_timer_step`) is registered as the render table's bit-0 entry; it draws the round timer's countdown on the frames `DS_00104AF4` divides by `DS_001088D0`, and only runs once `0x4F37C` has set `DS_00104AEC` bit 0. `0x45B50` and `0x47B04` are slot `+0x0C` callbacks (`0x3531C` case 7) stored by `0x45C10` and `0x47BFC`, neither of which Ghidra has a function for; they are registered but stay unreachable until those two land. `0x496DC` and `0x4A928` (with `0x4AA6C`) are ported without a call site: their callers are the case-13 body and the mode-9 block of `0x49C78`, the named gap of spec §7.4. Findings: `0x45B94` hands `0x2A17C` the literal `0x1F874610` as a palette handle (no fixup covers it, `TODO(verify)`); `0x4A928`'s `[0x1088C6] >> 24` is the byte `0x1088C9`; `0x47B04`'s `+0x54` clears precede the `0x36870` calls they steer. Seven new checks in `test_fight.c` (`check_49z_*`, one line each in `test_fight`) and 48 single-site mutations caught (two equivalent ones dropped). `make verify` (worktree-local `_gap45` dumps) is unchanged — front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained at N = 3617, `symbols.h` idempotent. Remaining named gaps: `0x45C10`/`0x47BFC` and the reaction callbacks of their tables, `0x4A868` and the case-13/14 bodies, the mode-9 block, `0x476CF`, and the `0x2C3FC` voices (§45-A).

**gap46-fighterset (record §50-A)** ported the `0x36000`-`0x41000` fighter helper cluster: `0x36F10` (`fighter_36f10`, the winner-pose initializer, now called from `fight_health_sync`'s in-range arm, which the `+0x42` gate still makes unreachable: `| 0x820` on the word is bits 5 and 11), `0x3FDD8` (a `+0x1C` callback, with `0x3D0C0`), `0x406B4` (the eight-actor burst from the `0x107EF8` free list, with its `rng` draws in order), `0x40954` (the `+0x0C` callback with the five-actor burst, with `0x408E8`) and the pose setups the §49-U callbacks are stored by: `0x3EC20`, `0x3EF44`, `0x3FB88`, their caller `0x3E9A4` with `0x3E800` (and `0x3C12C`), `0x3E8E4` and `0x3E924`, plus the neighbours the disassembly exposed (`0x3ECF8`, `0x3ED78`, `0x3EDB8`, `0x40BBC`, `0x3FF08`). `0x18AF8` is a two-call stub (it falls into `0x18B04` with EAX = 1). Eleven of them are registered in `actors_init`; the AL results of the reaction-row callbacks are dropped as `0x3F3F4`'s are (the `0x34E2C` callers reload or overwrite AL: `0x3CF33`, and `0x35803` past `0x350D0`/`0x3531C`). The voices are the deferred idiom. `check_fighterset` covers every arm with sentinels; fifty live mutations each failed the suite; note that a stream start overwrites the pset word, so the hflip later steps read comes from the record's `+0x29` bit 6. `PR_ORACLE_REQUIRED=1 run_tests` green 3x (~1.8 s); `make verify` gates unchanged. Named gaps: `0x3FEF8`/`0x3FD30`/`0x3F7F4`/`0x3F85C`, the pool init/tick `0x40608`/`0x407EC` (so `0x406B4` has no live caller), `0x215B0`, the `0x1078E8` writer, and `fighter_state_37464`'s pointer-table TODO. (Supersedes the `gap40-posecb` gap list above for the setups, `0x3E9A4` chain and `0x3ED78`/`0x3EDB8`.)

**The menu/text cluster (record §49-X, branch `gap43-menutext`).** New `port/src/game/menu.{c,h}` ports the two menu drivers `0x2FA40` (`menu_run`, the blocking one) and `0x2FFC4` (`menu_step`, one step per frame, its state in `DS_00107414..DS_0010744C`), the title-screen redraw `0x2FE84` (`menu_title_draw`), and the helpers they need: the item lookup `0x2FE40`, the debug lines `0x2F940`, the debug entry widget `0x305FC` (which files a typed value into high-score table 2 through `0x2DCA0`), the key/pad layer `0x2EB80` (pending key or the `0x4B0`-tick idle test), `0x2EBF0` (key -> arrow/Enter/Esc bits through the layout block `DS_00101514` points at), `0x2EDE0`/`0x2EEC8` (pad plus key poll, the second clearing the key), the fatal-error stub `0x2EA68` and, in `input.c`, the repeat-timer setter `0x50146`. The stock `0xBCBDC` menu's game-frame arm, case `0x27`, is wired to `input_repeat_set` + `menu_step` (any result but 0/-5/-10 is the `0x65431` longjmp, left as a `PORT:` note); nothing in the port sets mode `0x27` (the raw's Enter arm in the int 16h loop and the seven mode-setter stubs `0x2CBC4..0x2CC54` are unported), so no oracle path enters it. `0x2FA40` has no ported caller and no reachable trigger (its only caller is the unregistered item stub `0x2CB94`, entered through a data dword); it is ported and tested, not wired. Two raw quirks are replicated: `0x2FA40` starts each redraw at the row `\v` + digit gave (`\v3` -> row 8) while `0x2FFC4`'s redraw resets it to 5 (`0x300F9`); and `0x2FFC4` reads its Esc through the `0x2EBF0` bit because `0x2EEC8` clears the key before `0x2EB80` looks, which makes its `key == 0x1B` returns dead. The tests fabricate a string-table image (the shipped one has no `?`/`\n`/`\v` string), register callbacks at fake code addresses and read each cell's font palette back to tell the highlight modes apart: 35 single-site mutations of `menu.c` fail the suite (three equivalent ones documented). Named gaps: the item stubs and their `0x1B084` callee, the Enter arm that sets mode `0x27`, `0x2F9CC`'s `DS_0010740C` store, the voices (`0x2C3FC(0x100)`), the `0x62003` error exit and the two `0x65431` longjmps.

**§50-B dedupe (gap47).** `0x2EB80`, `0x2EBF0`, `0x2EDE0`, `0x2EEC8` and `0x305FC` had been ported twice (`menu.c` in §49-X, `config.c` in §49-Y). Both were compared with the raw disassembly and found behaviourally identical except the PORT scratch for `0x305FC`'s stack record (`0x3FFFE00` vs `0x3900000`). The `config.c` versions stay, `menu.c` now calls them, and the duplicates plus their `menu.h` declarations are gone, so each address has one `/* 0xADDR` header. `check_menu_input` and `check_menu_widget` were folded into `test_cfg_helpers` (assertions not already there were ported). The ported-function count is unchanged; the derivation record has the details.

**gap48-triage1a (record §50-C).** Triage of `0x1A000`-`0x1BFFF`. Ported the key-config record functions `0x1AE20`/`0x1AE28` (`config_keys_apply_defaults`/`config_keys_apply`), `0x1AEE0` (`config_keys_pack`) and `0x1AF64` (`config_keys_load`) in `config.c`; `config_set_defaults` now calls `0x1AE20` (previously mislabelled a "screen setup" no-op) and `game_init` runs the `0x20CF2`/`0x20CF9` pack/load pair, so the BIOS key-config record is no longer left zero for `config_key_flags`. Classified, with evidence in the record, as host-owned: the DPMI calls `0x1ACA8`, `0x1ACF0`, `0x1AD30`, `0x1AD64`, `0x1ADAC`, `0x1ADE4`; the ISR-side keyboard samplers `0x1B610`, `0x1B6A0`, `0x1B730`, `0x1B7C0` (a family of four: direction/button x player), `0x1B850`, `0x1B870`; the game-port reads `0x1B890`, `0x1B8DC`, `0x1B908`; and `0x1BBAC`. Deferred (EEPROM/config file, spec section 7): `0x1AFE8` (load) and `0x1B084` (save). Two wrong comments in `flow.c` (`0x1ACA8` "memory detect", `0x1B084` "resource free") were corrected. New `ch_check_keycfg` in `test_game.c`; 10 mutations fail the suite. `make verify` unchanged: front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained, `symbols.h` idempotent.


**The `0x1C000`-`0x1EB00` triage batch (record §50-D, branch `gap49-triage1c`).** Twenty-six unported functions were disassembled, their callers read from the Ghidra bridge, and classified: 11 are host-owned (the DPMI heap and its front ends `0x1C308`, `0x1C320`, `0x1C884`, `0x1C8CC`, `0x1E2A0`, `0x1E2D8`, `0x1E62C`, `0x1E6D8`, `0x1E774`; the audio work-buffer allocator `0x1D0BC`; the fatal exit `0x1D290`), each with the port's replacement named in the record's table. Seven were already ported under non-standard headers and now carry the standard header: `0x1C3A0` (`render_splice`), `0x1C3D0` (the unlink half of `render_list_remove`), `0x1C3FC` (`render_list_sort`), `0x1C470` (`gfx_flush_palette`, with two raw quirks left as `TODO(verify)`), `0x1C528` (a byte-identical second copy of `0x14268`, served by `sprite_node_build`), `0x1E75C` and `0x1E808` (`string_lock`/`string_unlock`). Eight were ported: the volume setters `0x1CAB8`/`0x1CED4` (`sound_music_volume`/`sound_sfx_volume`, now called by `attract_config_volumes*` in place of their inlined stores), the pause module `0x1D1B0`, `0x1D220`, `0x1D250`, `0x1D270`, the quit prompt `0x249F0` (`game_quit_prompt`, unwired: the frame loop keeps its ESC arm because `0x24C5C`'s key dispatch is not ported; its longjmp arm ends through the quit flag) and `0x2C9B8` (`config_voice_gate`). The unported count moved 715 -> 730 of 1203. The heavily called `0x1C884`/`0x1C8CC` turned out to be `malloc`/`free` for the Watcom runtime (31 of 34 call sites), not game list helpers.

**gap50-triage10 (record §50-E)** triaged the unported functions in `0x10000-0x14FFF` and `0x18000-0x19FFF` (28 addresses). Three were false positives ported under non-standard headers and are now normalised after a check against the raw: `0x12658` (`game_state_3_handoff`), `0x14268` (`sprite_node_build`) and `0x14328` (`render_list`; the raw zero-extends `DS_00107A3E/4E/3A/38` and uses the `sbb`-corrected projection, which the port had cast `(s16)` with an uncorrected shift and a `TODO(verify)`, now fixed and resolved, no frame effect). Three are ported: `0x18428` and `0x18460` (the anchor pre-dispatch `0x18540` calls; the dispatch table `0x1840C` is seven `0x18408` RETs, so both are effect-free; `fighter_18460` returns whether the dispatch is reached, for the test) and `0x19958`, the round-end debris spawner. Its caller `0x19B90` has no Ghidra function; it is update-table entry 2 (`0xA864C`), armed by `0x19820` at every fight start, and was never run by the port: it is now ported (`debris_update`, counts `DS_00100CA8` down, spawns, walks the `0x100C28` list, restarts landed debris streams and tears them down) and registered. The remaining 22 are documented, not ported: 9 belong to the movie-audio streaming unit that §49-W.2 defers (AIL driver open/close, stream reset/close, the 250 Hz timer), and 13 are host-owned DOS plumbing (DPMI memory and lock calls `0x1074B..0x109CA`, file size `0x109F4`, and the CD-ROM drive locator `0x10B70/0x10C30/0x10D0C/0x10D34`, replaced by `--game-dir`); `flow.c`'s note that called the CD functions DPMI locks is corrected. The classification table with evidence is in the record. New `check_debris_update` and `check_anchor_dispatch` in `test_fight.c`; 14 single-site mutations fail the suite (one equivalent, one hangs). Named gap: `0x1ADAC`/`0x1ADE4` were not read.

**The high-score rank probe `0x1ECC8`/`0x1EC38`/`0x2DDE4`, closing mode `0x1E`'s states 0/4/7 (record §49-R).** `0x2DDE4` (177 B) — the insertion-rank walk that compares a value, packed big-endian, against a high-score table record by record through the already-ported `hiscore_locate` (`0x2DB58`, record §46-A), returning the count of records it strictly beats or `-1` when it beats none — is ported as `hiscore_rank_probe` (`config.c`). Its two callers, `0x1ECC8` (`hiscore_rank_pair`) and `0x1EC38` (`hiscore_rank_single`, both `flow.c`), rank a fighter's post-match score (`DS_001077EC`/`DS_00107880`) against table 0; `hiscore_rank_pair` additionally rejects the one pathological tie the raw itself special-cases — both fighters landing on rank 9, the table's single last slot. `game_mode_1e_step`'s states 0, 4 and 7 (record §49-H) are rewired from "parked" PORT notes to the raw's real logic: state 0's existing short-circuit now continues into the real pair-rank gate, picking the higher score as the winner (unsigned, ties favour side 0) and storing state `0xB`/`0xC`; states 4 and 7 each run the real single-rank gate and then a **second, independent** gate (`DS_00104AD4`/`DS_00104B14`/`DS_00104ABC`) that was not visible from record §49-H's own header comment and was found only by reading the raw straight through past the probe call — a qualifying score alone is not sufficient to advance. Two callees past the gate stay out of scope: `0x1ED2C` (320 B, called from all three states) and, inside `0x1EC38`, `0x204F4` (708 B) — both exist solely to prime the still-unported `0x1F458` initials-entry screen (2897 B, explicitly out of this task's own scope), both discard their own return value in the raw, and neither writes anything any ported code reads back, so omitting them is a named gap with no observable effect, not a guess; `0x1ED2C`'s own callees were read and confirmed already-ported regardless, ruling out "too hard to check". A genuine raw quirk is replicated rather than smoothed over: table 0's ten records exactly fill the bytes before table 1's own base, so a probe that ties or loses through all ten does not stop there but compares one phantom "eleventh record" against table 1's leading bytes before its own byte budget goes negative — verified this resolves to `-1` for every value any real caller ever probes, and left untested against tables 1/2's own boundaries since no shipped caller ever passes `table != 0`. New `check_hiscore_rank` (`test_game.c`) exercises the probe directly against the fresh-CMOS table's real descending values (500000 down to 100); `test_fight.c`'s `check_mode_1e_gates` two former "stays parked" scenarios become real non-qualifying-score gate failures landing on the same next state the short-circuit reaches, and the new `check_mode_1e_rank_gate` covers both winner directions, the exact-tie-favours-side-0 case, the both-rank-9 rejection (read back off `DS_001044C0`/`DS_001044C2`), the winner re-probe's `DS_001044D6`/config-field-`0x26` side effects, and all four combinations of states 4/7's second gate, including a qualifying score that still bails. Proved live with three single-site mutations (the rank probe's strict-win test, state 4's second-gate term, the rank-pair's tie constant), each caught and reverted. `PR_ORACLE_REQUIRED=1 run_tests` is green three times (plus the three mutation round-trips), ≈1.7-1.8s each, no SIGBUS, no hang — the rank probe's one loop is bounded by the table's own byte budget going negative. `make verify` (worktree-local `*_DUMP`/`TITLE_PIN_DIR` overrides, `_gap37` suffixed) is unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained at N = 3617, `symbols.h` idempotent) — expected, since mode `0x1E` is reachable only from a live match's post-challenge high-score flow, which none of the no-input oracle windows reaches. Named gaps remaining: `0x1ED2C`/`0x204F4` (the name-entry screen's own setup, out of scope with `0x1F458` itself) and the deferred `0x2DAE4` audit adds inside `0x1EC38` (the established spec §7 idiom); states 5/8/0xB-0xE, primed by 0xF/0x10, remain parked exactly as record §49-H left them.

**Mode `0x1E`'s states 0xF/0x10, the name-entry arm `0x204F4` and the `0x1ED2C` resets (record §51-A).** `0x204F4` (537 B, not 708 B as §49-R said; its one caller is `0x1EC38` at `0x1ECA7`) is ported as `nameentry_arm(rank, score)` in `nameentry.c`, and `hiscore_rank_single` now calls it for a qualifying score. It builds a ten-record insertion preview in its stack frame that nothing reads back; the port keeps it because each `0x2DBC4` read leaves its decode in `DS_00105EFC`. It then fills a blank candidate name `DS_00104367` with factory record `rank`'s name (`0xA7BC0 + rank*0x2C`, raising `DS_0010431E`), clears the name buffers, stores the score at `0x104390`, and sets the timer (`0x2EE`) and the entry geometry: 18 letters at column 2 for rank 0, otherwise 3 at column 0x12, both on row 0x16. These are the geometry words §49-T.7 named as the gap. States 0xF (`0x1EFA2`) and 0x10 (`0x1F094`), read from the jump table `0x1EE6C`, store `DS_00104B25 = 0xE`/`0xD`. They reset the screen (`nameentry_reset`), re-probe the other side's score through `0x1EC38`, and prime `0x1F458` for that side. Both returns are dropped by the raw, and `DL = 0xE` survives the `0x2C3FC` voice because it pushes and pops EDX. §49-R's PORT-noted `0x1ED2C` calls at states 0/4/7 (`0x1EEEA`/`0x1F1E2`/`0x1F316`) are wired too: their reason (an unported `0x1F458`) no longer holds. With all 17 states of `0x1EEB0` now running the raw's logic, only the voices (§45-A) and the `0x2DAE4` audit adds remain as PORT notes. `test_mode1e_rearm` (`test_game.c`) caught 16 of 16 mutations, and `check_mode_1e_parked` no longer lists 0xF/0x10. `make verify` exits 0 with every enforced number unchanged: front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained, and `symbols.h` regenerates byte-identically. `port_progress.py` reports 742 of 1203 (62%), and the README title is updated to match. Remaining gaps: `0x20860` (the keyboard letter queue), the `0x1044DC`/`0x1044E0` writers, the voices and the audit adds.

**gap52-pose-pool (record §52-A)** ported the callbacks the §50-A pose setups store and wired their setups: the `+0x18` hooks `0x3F7F4` (`0x3FB88`'s; the `+0x57 == 1` gate, then `0x18C14` on `0xC7720`/`0xC772A`) and `0x3FD30` (`0x3FF08`'s; the `+0x61` gate, `r == 1` -> 4/4 and `0x36870`, `r == 0` -> `+0x57 = 1`), the `+0x1C` callback `0x3F85C` (both sides' motion cleared, the other side anchored and restarted on `0xC9170`, the side lowered to the `0xBD882` landing word `- 0x3C0` and placed `+/-0x280` from the other, `0xE7DC4` and the `0x108080` actor's `0xE7E9E`, the other slot to `0x10/0xA/0`) and the `+0x0C` callback `0x3FEF8` (a context built and dropped: no effect). `0x3F7F4`/`0x3F85C`/`0x3FD30`/`0x3FEF8` are not Ghidra functions, so only `0x3FD30` and `0x40608` move the count (741 -> 743 of 1203, 62%). **Correction to §50-A:** `0x3FF08` does have a caller, `call 0x3FF08` at `0x40026` inside `0x3FFDC`, character 5's reaction-`0x23` callback (the dword at `0xA50E4`); `0x3FFDC` (the slot's `+0x4E` = `|0x187FC()| / 30`, flip-signed) is ported and registered, giving `0x3FF08` and its three callbacks a live path from `0x34E2C`. The confetti pool: `0x40608` (init: both sentinels, 32 nodes `0x107F00..0x10807F` appended to the free list, countdown 8, then `0x406B4`), `0x407EC` (update-table entry 14: the countdown firing `0x406B4` at 4 and 0, the walk that lands pieces on `0xE8F0A` and returns their nodes, `word +0x2C = 0x400000 / (+0x30 >> 16)` for the rest, the empty-list exit clearing its own bit 14) and its caller `0x40554` (update-table entry 13: the `0x108098` mover that at `0x3800` runs the pool init, kills the actor, starts the `0x105FC30` effect and `0x37D18`), both registered, so `fighter_406b4` has callers; the only setter of bit 13 is the unported animation-opcode target `0x40434`, so the pool is still unreached in a run. Character 1's `0x21` cluster: the reaction callbacks `0x21C84`/`0x21D10` (reactions `0x23`/`0x24`, dwords `0xA3CE4`/`0xA3CF8`), their `+0x18` hooks `0x21A88`/`0x21B74`, `+0x1C` callbacks `0x21B08`/`0x21BE8` (which call the already-ported `0x21994`, whose callers these are) and their shared `0x215B0` (the `0x3E800` twin on `0xA8220`/`0xA820C`). Tests: `test_pose_pool` in `test_fight.c` (direct post-conditions plus whole-state differentials; the mutation campaign is listed in §52-A.4; its two survivors are an equivalent mutant and the effect handle, which the fixture cannot observe). `make verify` green with the gate numbers unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained, `symbols.h` byte-identical). Named gaps: the animation-opcode targets in these poses' streams (`0x3FF90`, `0x3FEA8`, `0x40034`, `0x3FC08`, `0x3FCB0`, `0x3F77C`) and `0x40434`, `0x21694` (unreferenced), and the `0x407EC` zero divisor (a `PORT:` guard where the raw would fault).

**gap53-nameentry-input (record §53-A)** closed the two gaps §51-A.7 left on the name-entry screen. `0x20860` (132 B, one caller: `0x24D67` in `0x24C5C`'s int 16h loop) is ported as `nameentry_key` in `nameentry.c`. When the queue is not full it maps backspace, Enter and space to DEL/END/space (`0x1B`/`0x1C`/`0x1A`) and a letter of either case to `0..0x19`. Letters are recognised through the runtime's class table at `0x81C84 + (u8)(c + 1)` (bits 6/7; exactly the 52 letters), read from `mem[]`. Every other byte is dropped before `DS_001044D8` is touched. It stores at `(write + 1) * 4` before the `& 0xF`, so entry 16 is live, as the reader was known to be. A full queue stores nothing but still sets `DS_001044D8`. `game_frame` now runs the loop's mode-0x1E arm before the mode switch, as the raw does: it peeks and reads the port's input queue, latches `DS_00105F30` and hands each non-zero ascii byte to `nameentry_key`. So a typed key reaches `0x1F458` in the same frame, and ESC during name entry is consumed (the raw sends it to `0x20860`) rather than quitting. `host.c` maps Backspace (scan `0x0E`, ascii 8). **The counters `D(0x1044DC)`/`D(0x1044E0)` have no writer (raw wins).** Ghidra's xrefs, the image's complete fixup list (the only records naming `0x1044D9..0x1044E7` are the five reads in `0x1F458`), the widths of the neighbouring stores and the bounds of every indexed base below them all agree: they keep the loader's BSS zero, so the four autorepeat tests are false in the original too, and nothing is ported. `test_nameentry_input` (`test_game.c`) covers the key mapping, the class boundaries, the unmasked index, the full queue, the frame wiring (word mode compare, latch, drain, ESC, another mode) and typing end to end; 23 of 23 mutations fail it. `make verify` exits 0 with every number unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained, `symbols.h` byte-identical). `port_progress.py`: 745 of 1203 (62%). Remaining gaps: the rest of the int 16h loop (every other mode, and extended keys in mode 0x1E), the voices and the audit adds.

**gap54-anim-targets (record §54-A)** ported and registered the animation-opcode targets that the §50-A/§52-A pose streams reach and the dispatcher used to skip: `0x3FF90` (`0xD000` in `0x3FF08`'s streams `0xE7F2E`/`0xD4C66`: the side's slot `+0x54 = 2`, the record's `+0x36 = 0x300`, `+0x34` = the slot's `+0x4E`, which makes it the reader of what `0x3FFDC` writes, and `+0x44` from `0xC7734[other char]`), `0x3FEA8` (`0xD000`: the `+0x57 == 0` gate, then 2/0/4/4, `+0x61 = 0` and `0x36870` on the slot's record), `0x40034` (`0xD500` at those streams' end: both slots gate it, then its callee `0x3BD8C` (2/4/0 with the record's `+/-0x100`/`0x300`/`0xC7734` words), then `0xE6F34` or `0xD3F58` for chars 0/5), `0x3FC08` (`0xD100` in `0x3FB88`'s `0xE7D6E`: the side's type-`0x10` descriptor `0xC76F8`/`0xC770C` gets the `0x29C08` handle and its spawn goes to `DS_00108080[side]`), `0x3FCB0` (`0xD100` in `0x3F85C`'s `0xE7DC4`: the `0xBB100` spawn gated by `0x105B3A <= 1` and the other slot's `+0x52 == 0x10`), `0x3F77C` (`0xD500` at `0xE7DC4`'s end: `0xE7DF8`, 0x28A/0x19/+/-0xB4, `+0x57 = 3`, `0x39834(1 - side, +0x5F)`) and `0x40434` (`0xD000` in `0xD4FF8`: the `0xBB2CC` mover beside the record, the step word `0x1080B2 = (0x3800 - 0x1000) / (d / 0xE0)` (both `div` unsigned), bit 13, `0xD501E`). The confetti pool chain `0x40434` -> `0x40554` -> `0x40608`/`0x406B4` -> `0x407EC` is now complete and tested end to end, but `0xD4FF8` is `0xC92B0[5]`, read only by the unported finisher code at `0x377DD`, so a run still does not start it. **Correction to §52-A.5:** `0x3F77C`'s `0xD500` word is at `0xE7DF2`, not `0xE7DE6`. `0x21694` (the `0x3E8E4` twin, no reference of any kind) is ported unregistered and recorded as dead in the raw. None of these is a Ghidra function, so the count is unchanged (744 of 1203, 62%). Tests: `test_pose_pool` gained ten `check_p54_*` parts (direct post-conditions, whole-state references, the `0x3FFDC` -> `0x3FF90` chain, the pool chain, and a dispatch part that walks each raw site's bytes through `0x2BC30`). 84 mutations were run and all but one fail; the survivor, 3FEA8's `+0x8A = 0`, is equivalent because `0x36870` clears the same byte on both of its paths. `make verify` passes with every gate number unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight 0 unexplained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained, `symbols.h` byte-identical). Named gaps: the finisher flow that would start `0x40434`; the zero divisors of `0x40434` (new, `PORT:` guard) and `0x407EC` (kept; §54-A.4 records that the pieces start at the fighters' `+0x32 = 0x400` and move by at most 6 per tick, but no raw path is proven or excluded); and the voices `0x2C3FC(0x48)`/`(0xB8)`.

**gap55-keyloop (record §55-A)** closed the rest of `0x24C5C`'s int 16h keyboard loop (`0x24CFE..0x24EE7`), now ported whole as `game_key_loop` in `flow.c`. It is a `PORT:` split that `game_frame` calls at the raw's place, and it replaces §53-A's mode-0x1E-only loop. Each key, in every mode, sets the latch `DS_00105F30`. The mode word is re-read for each key, as the raw does, because Enter changes it. Mode 0x1E hands non-zero ascii bytes to `nameentry_key`. Otherwise Enter (ascii `0xD`) in mode 3 stores the word 0x27, the service menu (`0x24EE0`). ESC opens `0x249F0`: `QUIT TO DOS?` in mode 3, nothing in mode 0x27, `ABANDON CONQUEST?` elsewhere. Space pauses (`- PAUSED -`, string `0x1E8`, on row 0xF, with `0x1D250`/`0x1D270` around a wait for another space) except in modes 3 and 0x27 and in mode 0x17 under the hook `0x10E80`. The extended keys, in any mode, are `0x10` → `0x249F0(0)`, `0x1F` → `0x1D220` and `0x32` → `0x1D1B0`. Every compare is the raw's unsigned single-value test. The fourth extended key, `0x24` (Alt-J), calls `0x5004A`, the joystick calibration. That function times the PIT (`0x50021`) and the game port, and its outputs are read only by the ISR sampler `0x1BBAC`'s helpers, so it and `0x50021` are classified host-owned (`tools/port_classification.txt`) rather than ported. `game_loop` no longer quits on ESC directly; it keeps the window-close quit, which now also ends a blocking prompt or pause (`PORT:` in `input_get_key`, `game_quit_prompt` and the pause). `host.c` sends Alt+letter as the BIOS scan code with ascii 0. `test_key_loop` (`test_game.c`) covers the wiring, Enter and the per-key mode re-read, ESC's three arms, the pause and its four exemptions, and the extended keys; 38 of 39 mutations fail it. The survivor (dropping the `DS_00104B22 = 1` store) is equivalent in the port. `make verify` exits 0 with every number unchanged (front-end 517/801/3/2, demo-fight fully explained at N = 1886, attract2 0 unexplained, `symbols.h` byte-identical). `port_progress.py`: 745 of 1203 (62%); portable 709 of 743 (95%). Remaining gaps: ESC's `ABANDON CONQUEST` yes is a `longjmp` back to `0x20C10`'s `setjmp` (a restart) that the port turns into a quit (spec §7), `0x5004A` (host-owned), the voices and the audit adds.

**all-gaps Task 2: the `TODO(verify)` sweep (record `2026-09-29-todo-verify-derivations.md` §1–§31).** Every one of the 31 `TODO(verify)` sites in `port/src` (ledger §C) was settled from the raw; `grep -rn 'TODO(verify)' port/src` now finds 0. Six are fixes, each with a test that failed first and a mutation that fails it again. The palette flush `0x1C470` clamps first + the flag dword into the flag dword, never the count, and writes as a do-while with the DAC index wrapping; `palette_record` stores the flag byte only (§3). A failed `AIL_init_sequence` sets status 2 and stops the engine without note-offs (`0x6A42E`, `0x69372`; §19). The AIL sample loop count is a count (0 forever, default 1 once; `0x6F120`), and the announcer, voice `0xCD` with loop byte 0, keeps the default as `0x1CB18` does (§23). `0x37464` indexes the table `DSD(0x1078DC)` points at (§29). `0x3C480` loads slot `+0x2C` before its `0x2BC30` call (§30). `MIXER_OPL_RATE` is static-asserted against the vendored core (§12). Sixteen are confirmed as-is, among them: `0x2D62C` is not a vector but the AIL 60 Hz timer callback `0x1BDF4`'s call (§1); the sequencer's 120 Hz is AIL preference 10, since `SBPRO2.MDI` sets its own service rate to -1 (§15); all 42 raw stores to `DS_00104AE4` are registered or no-op hooks (§20); `0x1F874610` is a real 31-colour palette handle (§31). Seven are `PORT:` reclassifications: the SDL failure branches and the unnamed AIL rows 21–23. Two are named gaps with evidence. The first is the driver's sustain (ctrl 64, `0x39CC`/`0x3B1E`, §13), sent only by `S16KONSD`/`S16SOUND` and so unreachable in the title-only port. The second is credit-countdown coverage, which needs a coin-input capture (§24). The sweep also found AIL's FOR/NEXT loop controllers 116/117, which are not modelled (§14; the title bank has no 117). `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A. The front-end/demo-fight/cycle-2 and attract/title frame dumps are byte-identical to `1a287b0`'s. The suite rises from 12532 to 12551 assertion sites (+19 new; three existing §3 assertions now expect the raw values: the DAC wrap and the flag-byte store), and `port_progress.py` is unchanged: 745 of 1203 (62%), portable 709 of 743 (95%).

**all-gaps Task 3a: clusters K1 and K9 (record `2026-09-29-k1-k9-derivations.md` §K1.1–§K1.7, §K9.1–§K9.13).** K1: seven functions the port already implemented now carry one standard header each, and the counter sees them. `0x34B6C` (`fight_health_sync`), `0x3BDB0` (`fight_attack_ready`) and `0x38910` (`title_origin_reset`, which now calls `frontend_origin_zero` for `0x4F1D0` instead of inlining its two stores) only needed the header. `0x1C528` is `0x14268` byte for byte after relocation (only the rel32 of its `call 0x1B544` differs), so it shares `sprite_node_build`. Three were splits, each with a mutation-proven test. `0x4682C` is split from the shared `ai_pred_cmd_sign` (it and `0x467DC` differ only in the `0x1A5D4` branch sense, `0x4686E jne` vs `0x4681E je`). `0x51ED8` becomes `sprite_blit_aperture`: unlike `0x51E5C` it does not restore the node's `+0x14`, so the node keeps rows − clip_b (`0x51F2F`); its one caller's node is a dead stack local with zeroed clips. `0x33714` becomes `palette_record_flagged`, the flag-byte-1 append (`0x3371F`). One finding: `fight_health_sync`'s case 0x12 (`0x34CC7..0x34D21`, `0x3BDDC` → `0x18B04`) is still a `PORT:` arm; both callees are ported now, so a later cycle can close it. K9: the raw caller scan was redone for all twelve proposals, and all twelve are supported and classified. Host-owned: `0x501A3` (all 80 stores go through EBX = `0xA0000`; `gfx_present` replaces it), `0x4FB98` (int 10h; the SDL window), `0x4FF8F`/`0x4FFD8` (pure joystick axis readers; their inputs are written only by the host-owned `0x4FC05/0x4FCFD/0x4FDF5/0x5004A`) and `0x2D62C` (the ISR tick; host `g_tick`). Deferred: the audit chain `0x2E180`/`0x2E0A4`/`0x2E034` (reached only from the deferred `0x2E934`), `0x2D498` (reached only from the no-op `0x2D4EC`), `0x32B94`/`0x32BB0` (their only callee is the deferred `0x2DAE4`) and `0x2DF8C`. Two corrections to the ledger. For `0x2DF8C`, the runtime callee `0x61A70` is memset, and on the port's path (`EBP = 1` at `0x2D82D`) it zeroes the 47 bytes `0x105ECD..0x105EFB`; those bytes are zero in the image and no ported code writes them, so the deferral stays inert. For `0x2D62C`, `0x1BFA4` is a `0x109A0` lock push, not a read. The unreferenced sampler routines `0x1B934..0x1BB73` get no row, because the counter only sees Ghidra functions. `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A, and an 8000-frame headless dump is byte-identical before and after (16000 `.idx`/`.pal` files, 0 differ). The suite rises from 12551 to 12569 assertion sites (+18). `port_progress.py`: 752 of 1203 (63%), portable 716 of 731 (98%).

**all-gaps Task 3b, K2: four trivial functions (record `2026-09-29-k2-k5-derivations.md` §K2.1–§K2.4).** `0x29B70` and `0x32968` are bare `ret`s. They are now `game_null_step` and `game_init_null` (`flow.c`), called from every raw site: `0x20E0B` in the fight reset, `game_frame` cases 1/2/0x20 (`0x2521A/0x25224/0x2522E`) and `game_init` (`0x20CC7`). `0x51F72` is `gfx_fill_screen`, the 0xC8 × 0x140-byte dword fill, and `gfx_screen_reset` now fills both `mem[]` buffers through it; its third call, on the aperture (`0x52151`), stays the `g_aperture` fill under a `PORT:` note (the aperture rule). `0x2D4B4` is `config_codeword_len`. **Correction (raw wins):** the ledger called it "the smallest power of two ≥ n+1". The loop jumps back to its `inc eax` (`0x2D4C1` → `0x2D4BA`) and returns EAX, so the result is n + r + 1 for the smallest r with 2^r ≥ n + r + 1, a SEC-DED codeword length (0x26 → 0x2D). All its callers (`0x2D4EC`, `0x2D6F8`'s stored-image arm, `0x2DAE4`) are no-op, unrun or deferred in the port, so nothing is rewired and it is tested directly. Each function has a mutation-proven test (the bare `ret`s: the data object filled with 0xA5 must hold it). `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A, and an 8000-frame headless dump is byte-identical before and after (16000 `.idx`/`.pal` files, 0 differ). The suite rises from 12569 to 12581 assertion sites (+12). `port_progress.py`: 756 of 1203 (63%), portable 720 of 731 (98%).

**all-gaps Task 3b, K5: `0x2EA74` falls into `0x2EA78` (record `2026-09-29-k2-k5-derivations.md` §K5).** **Correction (raw wins):** `menu.c` called `0x2EA74` "`xor eax,eax; mov eax,eax`, a no-op". Those 4 bytes (`31 C0 8B C0`) have no `ret`, so execution falls into `0x2EA78` with EAX = 0, and the function is `config_screen_wait(0)`: the key latch cleared, one frame built and presented, then two passes that each wait a tick and drain the BIOS key queue. It is now `config_screen_wait_zero` (`config.c`), and the three ported call sites use it: `menu_run` at entry (`0x2FA61`) and at the end of every poll pass (`0x2FE2F`), and `menu_step`'s init arm (`0x2FFF1`). This changes behaviour on a live path, the service menu (`game_frame` mode 0x27). No oracle capture enters that mode, so the evidence is the unit tests: `config_screen_wait_zero` directly (latch 0x77 → 0, ticks 5000 → 5002, the buffers swapped, a queued `'a'` latched and stamped at 5001), `menu_step`'s init (ticks 1000 → 1002) and `menu_run` (entry plus one completed pass = 4 ticks; an Esc exit before any pass = 2). Each site's removal fails its assertion. Because `0x2EA78` pumps the pad (`0x500C4`, `0x2EB0C`), the menu fixture `mt_press` now also sets the key bitmap (`+0x2D8/0x2D9`) and the previous raw word `DS_000E1C30`, a key held since the last pump. With the old fixture the pump drops the press and `menu_run` never returns. No existing assertion changed. The other 15 raw call sites are in unported service-menu code and stay with it. `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A, and an 8000-frame headless dump is byte-identical before and after (16000 files, 0 differ). The suite rises from 12581 to 12597 assertion sites (+16). `port_progress.py`: 757 of 1203 (63%), portable 721 of 731 (99%).

**all-gaps Task 3c, K3: `0x38990` runs every frame (record `2026-09-29-k3-k8a-derivations.md` §K3).** `0x38990` is now `render_scroll_track` (`render.c`). It sets `DS_00107A3C` to the low word of `DS_000F0AEC` with only its low byte masked by `0xC0` (`0x38997`), and `DS_00107A4A` to `DS_000F0AEC` divided by 64 through the truncating `sar`/`shl`/`sbb` idiom, plus the zero-extended `DS_00107A4E`, keeping the low 16 bits (`0x389BC`). `game_frame` calls it where the raw does. That is `0x24CC8`, plus `0x24CC3` first when `DS_00104B26 != 0`, a byte nothing stores. Both calls come after the `0x24C73` AI block and before the frame counter. The old "deferred" comment stood after the update table, which was the wrong place. The raw scan finds one reader of both words, `0x389C4` (`render_scroll_edge`). `DS_00107A3C` has no other writer. The other two writers of `DS_00107A4A` (`0x38730`, `0x38910`) were already ported at their own sites, so nothing is written twice. Until now `DS_00107A3C` stayed 0, so `render_scroll_edge` always took its `< 1` arm. Over `--check 8000`, a temporary counter saw `DS_000F0AEC` in `[0, 5888]` during the demo fights and the `>= 1` arm taken 725 times. That arm changes `DS_00107A38`. It cannot move `DS_00107A4C` on these runs: `F0AEC >= 0` makes `4A >= 4C`. The port reads `DS_00107A38` only for a layer-2 sprite that follows no layer-1 sprite, and no such sprite appears on these runs. So the 8000-frame dump is byte-identical before and after (16000 files, 0 differ), and so is the front-end/demo-fight/attract2 dump. The oracles cannot see this state change. Tests: the direct body (three seeds pin the dword divide, the truncation and the low-byte mask, and four neighbouring words survive), and a mode-1 `game_frame` that must write both words. Both are mutation-proven. `make verify` exits 0, and every oracle line equals ledger §A. The suite rises from 12597 to 12611 assertion sites (+14). `port_progress.py`: 758 of 1203 (63%, unchanged), portable 722 of 731 (99%).

**all-gaps Task 3c, K8a: update-table entry 6 `0x25FAC` (record §K8a).** The entry was unregistered, so `run_process_table` skipped it without a word, even though modes 5 (case 2, `0x25E1D`), 0x23 (`0x26B2C`) and 0x30 (`0x294C9`) arm bit `0x40` of `DS_00104AE8` together with the card record `DS_00104ACC`. It is now `flow_card_ramp_step` (`flow.c`), registered in `actors_init`. Each frame it adds 1 to the card's byte `+0x2D`, so the word `+0x2C` grows by `0x100` with no carry out of it. Once the zero-extended word is `>= 0x1000`, it is clamped to `0x1000` and bit `0x40` is cleared with a byte store. The descriptors start the word at `0x0010`, so the ramp takes 16 frames, and the card is killed after the 60-frame countdown. The raw never tests `DS_00104ACC` for zero, and neither does the port. No oracle path reaches modes 5/0x23/0x30, so the evidence is the unit tests. They cover the table dword `0xA865C`, `fn_resolve`, four body cases (below the clamp, at the clamp, the unsigned compare on `0x8010`, the byte wrap on `0xFF10`) and one `game_frame` whose dispatcher runs the entry. Each case is mutation-proven. `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A, and the dumps are byte-identical. The suite rises from 12611 to 12626 assertion sites (+15). The counter does not move, because Ghidra has no function at `0x25FAC`.

**all-gaps Task 3d, K4: the effect proximity gate `0x4A868` (record §K4).** `0x4A868` is ported as `fight_4a868` (`fight.c`). With R the entry's actor, it returns 1 when `|0x2BE00(R) - entry+0x14| <= |2 * (R's dword +0x32 >> 16)|`: the shift is arithmetic, so the reach is the signed `+0x34` word, both sides are made non-negative with 32-bit `neg`, and `setle` makes the edge inclusive. It has six raw call sites, and five are now wired. `0x4BF18` (`fight_4bf18`) case 1 with bit 5 set calls it at `0x4BFDE`, and its gated body `0x4BFEB..0x4C034` is ported with it: the `+0x38/+0x34/+0x36` words cleared, type 8, `+0x55 = 1` and the `0xC958C[index]` stream at 3.0. That body's voice `0x2C3FC(0xC8)` is a case-2 sample, so it stays a `PORT:` note (§45-A, K12). `0x4DEF4` (`fight_effects_idle_pass`) calls it in each of states 1..4 (`0x4DF8E`/`0x4DFFA`/`0x4E066`/`0x4E0D0`), and the four state bodies are ported: `DS_001088BB = 1`, `+0x34 = 0`, a stream chosen by R's signed `+0x30` high word against the zero-extended `DS_000BD898` (the `>=` side also sets `+0x55 = 1`), 3.0 or, in state 4, `0xC9724` at 5.0, then state 0. The raw corrects the old header, which put the `+0x55` store on the low branch. The sixth site, `0x4A361`, is inside the case-14 body of `0x49C78`, which K13 ports later, so it stays unwired and its comment cites §K4.4. No oracle path moves: the 8000-frame dump (16000 files) and the front-end/demo-fight/attract2 dump are byte-identical, and `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A. New `check_fx_gate` in `test_fight.c`: 8 direct cases, 8 idle-pass scenarios (open and shut) and the `fight_4bf18` pair. All 26 mutations fail the suite. Assertion sites 12626 -> 12658 (+32). `port_progress.py`: 758 -> 759 of 1203 (63%), portable 722 -> 723 of 731 (99%).

**all-gaps Task 3d, K6: the voice wrappers `0x4F714` and `0x4F728` (record §K6).** `0x4F714` is `sound_voice_stage` (`flow.c`): `sound_voice(word 0xC9888[stage])` as a tail jump, so its AL is the dispatcher's. Stages 0..7 name the case-1 music records `0x20, 0x21, 0x1B, 0x1C, 0x1E, 0x1D, 0x1F, 0x1F`. `0x4F728` is `sound_voice_match_end`. It plays `0xDF` when the match result `DS_00104AD4` (a dword) is neither -1 nor 3, that side's slot `+0x63` byte is 0 and the signed `DS_001088F2` is above 0, and `0x23` otherwise, then `0x22`. The caller's EAX is never read. Both replace the `PORT:` notes at their one raw call site each: `0x25C09` in `game_hook_25bbc` and `0x27E4B` in `flow_match_end`. Every voice they can play is case 1 or case 5 in the shipped table. Case 1 stores `DS_00105D5C` and `0x1CA14`'s song words, and with no sequence handle it stops there. Case 5 `0x22` then finds a handle in `DS_00105D5C` and keeps them. Neither case reads a resource, draws or draws rng, so wiring changes those three words, as the raw does, and no pixel. A probe build shows that neither caller is entered in `--check 8000`. The 8000-frame and front-end/demo-fight/attract2 dumps are byte-identical, and `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A. New `check_voice_wrappers` in `test_game.c` covers three stages, ten gate cases and the trailing `0x22` with its order, using a test-only retype of `0x23`'s handle to `0x1B`. There are also two wiring checks in `test_fight.c`. All 13 mutations fail the suite. Assertion sites 12658 -> 12669 (+11). `port_progress.py`: 759 -> 761 of 1203 (63%, README unchanged), portable 723 -> 725 of 731 (99%).

**all-gaps Task 3d, K7: the sample start `0x1CB18` and `0x1CC28`'s slot choice, derived but not ported (record §K7).** Both bodies are derived from the raw. `0x1CB18` resolves the slot's queued handle, copies `[resolved]` bytes into the slot's `+0x10` buffer, and then makes the AIL calls init, address, volume, rate `0x2B11`, type (0, 0), loop count 0 when the loop byte is 1, and start. It then moves `+0x04` to `+0x0C`. `0x1CC28` queues a sample larger than `0x6000` bytes only on slot 0. A smaller one goes on the first free slot from 3 down to 0, where free means a buffer, no queued handle and not playing, or else on the slot with the oldest `0x500BB` time, which is ended and re-inited. Every AIL call maps onto the port's `ail.h` one for one, but a faithful port needs three decisions beyond the AIL boundary. First, the `+0x10` buffers: their only writer is `0x1D0BC`, which is classified host-owned, so the port has a DIG driver and no buffers, a state the raw never reaches. Second, the `0x500BB` clock `DS_00101500`, which only the timer ISR advances. Third, the title announcer stand-in, which drives slot 0's handle outside the slot records. The cluster is therefore left for a re-plan (§K7.5), as the controller's stop rule for K7 requires. The named-gap comments in `flow.c` now cite §K7. Once `0x1CB18` is wired, the two samples of voice `0x4D` would start at f = 4444 in a windowed run.

**all-gaps Task 3e, K8c: the remaining update-table entries 4, 8, 9, 11, 12 and 17 (record §K8c).** All six are ported from the raw and registered in `actors_init`, together with entry 8's handle pick `0x29C20` (the one Ghidra function here). The code is in `fighter.c`: `fighter_37c8c`, `fighter_34648` + `fighter_29c20`, `fighter_3800c`, `fighter_4f890`, `fighter_24150` and `fighter_45d98`. `fighter_37b54` (`actors.c`) is exported for entry 17's tail. Before porting, a scan of every writer of `DS_00104AE8..AEA` found each bit's setter (§K8c.0). Entry 4's bit 0x10 has no setter in the image and its slot pointer `DS_001078E0` has no writer, so it is dead code. Entry 8 is set by the ported stun start `0x34168`. Entry 11 is set by the ported `0x4F944`, which corrects ledger §B.2: `or ah,8` at `0x4F969`. Entries 9, 12 and 17 are set only by the unported animation-opcode targets `0x37EA0`, `0x24078` and `0x45D58` (`0xD100` words in the character streams), so the port never arms them. These three setters are now named gaps (§K8c.7). A probe build that printed the update mask on every change saw only `0` and entry 7's `0x80`, both in `--check 8000` and in the front-end/demo-fight/attract2 driver. No K8c bit is armed on a no-input path. The closure is 1176 B across 7 functions, with no new callee. Entry 17's two voices stay `PORT:` notes (§45-A). The 8000-frame and front-end dumps are byte-identical, and `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A. The new `check_update_k8c` in `test_game.c` covers the table resolution and each entry's arms. All 57 mutations fail the suite; two needed a stronger case (the frame-word mask and the `rng(0xA)` range). Also in this task: the `0x467DC` header cites §K1.4, the §K3.2 test block is moved above its unrelated comment, and `SND_SLOT0_63`/`VW_SLOT63` use `DS_00107813`. Assertion sites 12669 -> 12778 (+109). `port_progress.py`: 761 -> 762 of 1203 (63%, README unchanged), portable 725 -> 726 of 731 (99%).

**all-gaps Task 5a: the mode-0x33 wiring, the bonus cards, the animation setters and `0x4F944`'s clamp (record `2026-09-29-e-wire-k8b-k8d-derivations.md`).** §W: mode 0x33's `0x29638` now calls `0x4DEF4` at `0x2965F` (`fight_effects_idle_pass`), between the second `fight_hud_pass` and `camera_y_commit`, the same call mode 0xF makes at `0x277E9`. Both raw callers of `0x4DEF4` are now wired; of `0x4A868`'s six sites only `0x4A361` (case 14, K13) is left. §B8: update entries 15 `0x260BC` and 16 `0x26194` are `flow_bonus_card_a_step`/`_b_step` (`flow.c`), registered in `actors_init`. Each is a three-state card: it grows its `+0x2C` word by 0x80 to 0x1000 (a zero-extended compare), holds it 0x3C frames, starts the exit stream `0xE91A4`/`0xE91D4` at 1.0, then shrinks it by 0x200 and kills it at zero, clearing its own bit. Entry 15's hold end also runs `0x2604C` for the side `0x27C48` stored in `DS_00104B1C` when that byte is not negative. §D8: the three animation-opcode targets that were the only setters of entries 9, 12 and 17 are ported (`fighter_37ea0`, `fighter_24078`, `fighter_45d58`) and registered through `anim_indirect`'s `(rec, arg)` wrappers. `0x37EA0` replaces the calling record with a static sprite (by character, `0x46B6..0x46BA`, the handle `0x0105FEBC`), arms entry 9's countdown and sets `DS_00104B0C`. `0x24078` restarts the other side's record, spawns `0xA84FC` and arms entry 12. `0x45D58` resets entry 17's twelve states. §C: `0x4F944`'s clamp is a signed `jle`, so a negative count is stored as it is (the port clamped it to 0x14). Carried minors: `FIGHT_ROUND_HI` replaces the duplicate `UPD11_ROUND_HI`; entry 17's state-3 sum is added as `u32`; `check_update_k8c` (d) now restores the palette table. A probe (not committed) found none of these paths on `--check 8000` or the `PR_FRONTEND_DET` run (§R). `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line unchanged, and the 8000-frame and front-end dumps are byte-identical. Assertion sites 12778 → 12897. The five new addresses are non-Ghidra, so `port_progress.py` stays `762 1203 63`.

**all-gaps Task 5b, K13: the inline blocks of the effects pass `0x49C78` (record `2026-09-29-k13-fx74-derivations.md` §K13.1–§K13.7).** The parts of `fight_effects_pass` that were left as named gaps are now ported inline, each with its raw range: the case-13 body `0x4A24A..0x4A345`, the case-14 body `0x4A346..0x4A45B` (both share the `0x496AC` tail `0x4A45C`), and the mode-9 block `0x4A476..0x4A590`. Case 13 walks the scattered crowd off. On screen (`+0x2A` bit `0x1000` with x in `[-0x300, 0x5700]`) it waits. An entry of side `(s8)DS_001088C9` is killed unless `DS_001088C7` is set. Otherwise the actor turns onto `0xC95D4[si]`, its target is the case record's `0x2BE00` term ± `rng(0xC00)`, and it becomes type `0x0E` while `0x496DC` spawns `DS_00108878` more (its only call site; the count is then zeroed). Case 14 counts itself and stops at its target through `0x4A868` (`0x4A361`, the last of its six sites to be wired): `+0x55 = 1` and the `0xC955C[si]` stream, because EDX = si*4 survives `0x4A868`'s push/pop. Otherwise it does case 13's wait, turn and target, without the kill or the spawn. The mode-9 block reads the frame locals the walk now keeps: the per-side counts, the entry count, and the type-9, case-14 and type-11 counters. From them it latches the crowd voice countdown `DS_001088B0` (`rng(0x14) + 0x78`, re-armed at 0, counted down; the voices stay `PORT:` notes, §45-A), sets `DS_001088C9` and `DS_001088B4`, runs the survey `0x4A928` when every entry idles (its only call site), and scatters the list to type 12 after a type-11 walk ends. **Corrections (raw wins):** the ledger's block ranges ended at the draw gates; the measured sizes are 252, 278 and 266 B. The "type-8 held body" `fight.h` listed was already ported (record §42-C, `0x4A08A..0x4A114`). The old cases 13/14 drew `rng(0xC00)` unconditionally, but the raw draws only on the turn path. One named gap is left. On a drawn round (`DS_00104B16 == 2`) the block reads the frame word `[ESP+4]`. Case 3's landing writes that dword as its hflip temporary (`0x4000` or 0, `0x49DF2`/`0x49DFE`), and the port now stores it too (fix round 1: the first version wrongly said it was never written). Only its value before the first case-3 store in a call is unpinned: it is uninitialised memory of `0x49C78`'s own frame, and the port uses 0. A probe (§K13.5) found no mode 9 and no type 9..14 on `--check 8000` or the `PR_FRONTEND_DET` run, so no oracle path reaches these blocks. New `check_k13_case13`, `check_k13_case14` and `check_k13_mode9` in `test_fight.c` add 118 assertion sites. They failed first against the old code (71 FAIL lines), and 33 of 33 mutations fail. The one early survivor, `r >= 0` in case 13's target, was killed by an added zero-term case. Three commits, one per block. `make verify` is green with the ledger §A lines unchanged; the 8000-frame and front-end dumps are byte-identical; the counter stays `762 1203 63` (blocks are not functions).

**all-gaps Task 5c, E-OPEN: the six open prose gaps (record `2026-09-29-e-open-derivations.md` §1–§6).** Two closures, two stale comments with pinning tests, and two re-scopes.
- **§2, row 14.** The slot `+0x42` bit `0x10` that `0x34B6C`'s position gate reads has exactly one raw setter: `0x36E78`'s `or edx,0x101000` (`0x36E9D`/`0x36EAF`). A scan of every Ghidra function plus the 80 KB of code between them found no other, and `0x33ACC`/`0x33B00` only save and restore it. `0x36E78` was already ported, so the `0x34BE8` arm (`0x36F10`) is reachable, and the "no writer" claims in `fight.c` and demo-fight §7.10 were wrong. The same false premise had left `0x3531C`'s `DS_001078F6` expiry half-skipped. Its retire `0x353CF..0x353DD` (`0x2B150` on `DS_001078EC`, gated on config bit 1) is now ported. RED: only that assertion failed.
- **§3, row 21.** `0x263F4`'s static closure has 315 functions. The only unported ones are 12 classified host-owned/deferred rows under `0x1B544`.
- **§4, row 24.** The slot `+0x24` table is `DS_000BDA8C[char]` (seven pointers), stored by the ported spawn core at `0x33D5D`. It is indexed by s in `[0, 0x4B0]`, 2 bytes per s. `check_health_table` pins all seven characters through the real spawn.
- **§5, row 25.** `DS_00100B54` for `check_unfreeze`'s fixture is 163: 7 rows x 37 bytes x 8 bits = 2072, then `0x1703E..0x1706F`. B18 is 7 from `0x15C30`'s clip.
- **§1, row 1, re-scoped.** The loader's tick drift is DOS I/O time in the runtime's `0x61C60`/`0x61CF6`/`0x61EC0`. No raw byte holds it, and no reader uses the absolute tick.
- **§6, row 27, re-scoped.** The loop-1973 s16title screen belongs to the front-end driver, which enters at state 2. The port's real boot reads entry 7 at f 3 through the raw's own `0x110D8`. The faithful driver fix would move the enforced attract2 dump count (2308 -> 2307) and its named splice, so it is not made.

Ten mutations all fail the suite. The headless 8000-frame dump is byte-identical before and after, and a probe shows that the no-input run never reaches the new arm. `make verify` exits 0 with every oracle line equal to ledger §A. The counter is unchanged at 762/1203.

**all-gaps K10 MOVIE-BLIT: `0x50D23` classified host-owned (record `2026-09-29-k10-movie-blit-derivations.md` §K10).** `0x50D23` is the movie player `0x1C740`'s dirty-rectangle blit. It is called once per rectangle from `0x1C82D`, with `{x0, y0, x0+w, y0+h}` taken from the Smacker handle's `+0x684..+0x690`. For each dword where the E87A4 buffer differs from the E87A0 buffer, it stores the new dword to the aperture (`0x50D4A add ebx,0xa0000`) and to E87A0 (EDI). There are 80 stores through each register and no other store, call or port I/O. A byte-level interpreter of its 4405 bytes matches the closed-form model on 308 rectangles. The ledger's "the EDI half is `mem[]` state" is corrected: the E87A0 buffer is the aperture's shadow. `0x52106(0)` at `0x1C74D` fills both with 0, `0x50D23` writes both together, and `0x52106(0)` at `0x1C873` zeroes it on every path before any reader. Its only reader in between is `0x50D23`'s own compare; the transitive call tree of `0x1C740` (281 functions) holds no other function that references `0xE87A0`, `0x1014E4` or `0x1014E8`. So the net effect is aperture = E87A4 over each rectangle. The port's full-frame `movie_present`/`gfx_present` reproduces that, as the smk oracle shows (120/120 and 41/41 against the original's screen). `test_movie_blit` pins both premises on twg playback: the aperture equals the E87A4 buffer at all 43 screens, and both buffers are zero with the pointers unswapped at exit.
