Three mutations of `movie.c` fail it. The one named residue is that a rectangle leaving the 320x200 frame would store past the buffer in the original; the rectangles come from the runtime `0x64ED8`, and no oracle observes this. `make verify` is green with every oracle line equal to ledger §A. The counter's ported figure is unchanged at 762/1203, and the portable figure is 726/730.

**all-gaps K11 cycle 1: the options-menu shell `0x2CB74..0x2CC54` and `0x2F99C` (record `2026-09-29-k11-service-menu-derivations.md` §K11.0–§K11.2).** A new module, `svcmenu.c`, ports ten functions. `0x2CB74` is MAIN MENU "Start": it runs `menu_step` over the START MENU `0xBCCCC`, with flags 0, and re-initialises the one shared `0x2FFC4` state. `0x2CB94` is "GAME OPTIONS": the blocking `menu_run(0xBCC1C)`, then `config_credits_init`, with the deferred CMOS writer `0x1B084` as a `PORT:` note. The seven START MENU setters store modes `0x28..0x2E` and sub-modes 0..4 into `DS_00104B00` (a word) and `DS_00104B1D`. `0x2F99C` is the screen reset. `svcmenu_register()` registers the nine table callbacks, and `game_init` calls it once. `game_init` also gains the two `0x2F9CC` stores the port lacked: `DS_0010740C = 0x1D2D0` (`0x2FA01`; `[0x1D2D4]` is the CONFIG OPTIONS table `0xA2EB4`) and `DS_00107410 = field 0x2A & ~3` (`0x2FA10..0x2FA1C`). **Correction (raw wins):** an Esc on the START MENU returns -5 (`0x30466`: the nested state has flags 0 and `cur != count`), not the -1 the plan's Review Focus names. A new test seam, `host_set_pump_hook` (`PORT:`, NULL in the game), lets `test_svcmenu` script one key per presented frame. The harness exits the process instead of hanging when the hook is dead (mutation 8). All eight mutations fail the suite. `make verify` is green, with the oracle lines unchanged. The 8000-frame headless dump (`.idx` and `.pal`) is byte-identical before and after. The counter is unchanged at 762/1203, because none of the ten is a Ghidra function.

**all-gaps K11 cycle 2: the option editor, CONFIG OPTIONS, SOUND TEST and MUSIC TEST `0x2CC74`..`0x33578` (record `2026-09-29-k11-service-menu-derivations.md` §K11.3).** `svcmenu.c` gains nine functions. `0x2CD30` draws one option row: the label at column 4, then the value's 1-based index (the runtime `itoa` `0x655E4` is formatted in C, a `PORT:` note), its text and its string on the next row. A `'*'` text marks the default and switches the mode to `0x1000`. `0x2CC74` draws or releases seven rows (3..21). `0x2CF00` is the blocking editor. It has a window of 6 with the "+ MORE +" markers (`0x72`, their ends replaced by `0x19`/`0x3B`), Up/Down, and Left/Right that wrap the field. A pad button (`0x4000000`, which reaches the poll through `0x50161`'s unmasked level) restores the entry bits. Enter returns the bits; Esc returns -1 when CL is set. `0x2CACC` hands `[[DS_0010740C]+4]` = `0xA2EB4` to `0x33578`, the CONFIG OPTIONS screen over config field `0x29`. `0x30EB4`/`0x30F54` loop the editor over `0xA3500`/`0xA3060` and play each pick through `0x2C9E8`/`0x2C9CC`. `svcmenu_register` adds the three table callbacks. **Corrections (raw wins):** field `0x29` is a 32-bit field (descriptor `0x1D980`), not bits 0..26. Its bit 15 is the option "Restore Factory Default?" (record 9), which `0x33578` honours both before the edit and after it (`0x33674..0x33695`, missing from the plan). The rows run 3..21, not 3..0x16. One named gap: a language change is stored but not shown, because the port does not run `0x47370`'s reload. `sm_check_options` (29 scripted frames) pins the rows, the editor's wraps, restore key, scroll and exits, the voice ids, and CONFIG OPTIONS end to end. All 21 mutations fail the suite. The `+0` read in `0x2CACC` fails it by a crash in the `0x32700` fallback. `make verify` is green, with the oracle lines unchanged. The counter is unchanged at 762/1203, because none of the nine is a Ghidra function.

**all-gaps K11 cycle 3: ADJUST VOLUME and 2 PLAYER HANDICAP `0x2F464`..`0x31138` (record `2026-09-29-k11-service-menu-derivations.md` §K11.4).** `svcmenu.c` gains five functions. `0x2F464` (`text_number_cont`) draws a formatted number at the text cursor. `0x30728` shows "ERROR SETTING VOLUME LEVEL" until Esc. `0x30864` is ADJUST VOLUME: three rows (music, field `0x35`; effects, field `0x37`; voice, field `0x2A` bits 0..1), with bars through `0x30788`. Left and Right step a volume by 8 (clamped to 0..0xFF) or the voice by 1 (0..3), and each change is applied at once through `0x1CAB8`/`0x1CED4`. A zero music level mutes through voice `0x22` and the byte `0xBD458`, and the fields are saved on Esc. `0x30FE8` draws one handicap row (0x32..0x96, a number and a 21-cell bar). `0x31138` is 2 PLAYER HANDICAP: it edits the key-config record's `+0x24`/`+0x26` in steps of 5, Enter restores them, and Esc stores them in `DS_00107468`/`DS_0010746C` and applies the record. `attract_config_volumes_unscaled` now returns field `0x35` as read (`0x2C9B1`), which `0x30864` tests; the fight callers still ignore it. `svcmenu_register` adds `0x30864` and `0x31138`. **Corrections (raw wins):** the layout bytes are three bytes above the plan's addresses, because each is read as a dword and shifted right by 24 (`[0xBD441] sar 24` is the byte at `0xBD444`). Field `0x35` is 16 bits wide (descriptor `0xE0C0`), so it never reads -1, and `0x30864`'s error arm `0x308CC` cannot be reached in the raw: it is ported but not tested. `0x30FE8` releases two rows, not four. `0x31138`'s redraw flag is the caller's EBP, which is `menu_run`'s stride 0x10, so the first pass redraws. `sm_check_volume` (44 scripted frames) pins the number layout, the error exit, the arrows (volume: Down, the Up wrap, the unclamped Left and Right steps on music, both clamps; handicap: Down, the unclamped Left and Right steps, both clamps), the scaled and unscaled volumes, mute and unmute, the saved fields, the handicap clamps, the Enter restore and the exit stores. The cycle-2 review items are folded in. The `0x2CACC` test now uses a scratch record, so the `+0` mutation fails a check instead of crashing. The Up/Down hold check is tested through a held pad bit (`sm_held`, 2 frames). The repeat words are restored, and two comments are corrected. All 42 mutations fail the suite. The 8000-frame dump is byte-identical before and after. The counter moves from 762 to 763 of 1203 (`0x2F464` is a Ghidra function).

**all-gaps K11 cycle 4: MODIFY CONTROLS and TEST CONTROLS `0x2EF48`..`0x32358` (record `2026-09-29-k11-service-menu-derivations.md` §K11.5).** `svcmenu.c` gains nine functions. `0x2EF48` (`text_hex_format`) formats a value in hex from the digit table `0x2EF10`, right-aligned, padded with `' '` or `'0'`, and returns the digit count; `0x2F48C` (`text_hex_set`) draws it. `0x314A0` draws a 3x3 stick indicator of `'+'`/`'.'` cells. `0x319B0`/`0x31A78` release or draw the four button-key names in a 2x2 block below a point, and `0x31B94`/`0x31C78` the four direction-key names in a cross around column `0xA` or `0x1E` (left and right unwrapped, down a column); each dispatches through a four-entry jump table, ported as a `switch`. `0x31F24` is MODIFY CONTROLS: Left and Right pick a player, Up and Down cycle its device (0 KEYBOARD, 2 4 BUTTON JOYSTICK, 4 2 BUTTON JOYSTICK, 6 KEYBOARD/JOYSTICK) within the limits the other player's device sets, and Esc applies the key-config record through `0x1AE28`. `0x32358` is TEST CONTROLS: it shows the live pad sticks and 'O'/'X' button markers until the latched Esc, and with `DS_00107410` bit 4 the diagnostic rows (ADDRESS/RAW DATA, the pad word in hex). `svcmenu_register` adds `0x31F24` and `0x32358`. **Corrections (raw wins):** Left/Right pick the player and Up/Down cycle the device (the plan had Left/Right cycling it); the `0x500BB` reads are a 12-tick wait after each Up or Down, not a blink; `0x2EF48` returns the digit count, not `width - digits`; `0x319B0` reads only EBX/ECX (the port drops the side argument), and `0x31B94` only EAX; the button names are a 2x2 block, not a diamond; the three diagnostic marker entries hold string pointers where ids belong, which decode as strings `0xAC`/`0xB0`/`0xB4` through the ENGLISH.TXT group ring. One named gap: the diagnostic RAW DATA row reads linear `0xFFE80003`, an arcade address outside `mem[]`; the port draws 0. `sm_check_controls` (23 scripted frames, K11 total 106 of 160) pins the formatter, the stick, the four name helpers, the device cycle with both wraps and an unlimited step each way, three limit arms (player 1 Up, player 2 Up and player 2 Down; neither player 1 Down arm is tested), the 12-tick wait (to 10..12 ticks), the player selection, the applied record, and both TEST CONTROLS arms. The cycle-3 review's two minors (the voice Left step and the unclamped effects Right step) are listed as not tested in §K11.4. All 46 mutations fail the suite. The 8000-frame dump is byte-identical before and after. The counter moves from 763 to 765 of 1203 (`0x319B0` and `0x31A78` are Ghidra functions).

**all-gaps K11 cycle 5: CONFIGURE KEYBOARD `0x19C60`..`0x19DF0` (record `2026-09-29-k11-service-menu-derivations.md` §K11.6).** `svcmenu.c` gains four functions. `0x19C60`/`0x19D34` set and get one of the sixteen key slots of the key-config record `0x100CAC` through two jump tables (ported as `switch`es): up, right, down, left and the four buttons of each player, a non-monotone map (slot 9 is `+0x1A`, slot 10 `+0x16`); a slot above 15 (unsigned) stores nothing and reads 0. `0x2EBBC` takes and zeroes the raw key word `DS_00105F28`. `0x19DF0` is CONFIGURE KEYBOARD: it packs the mirror into the global record, draws LEFT/RIGHT PLAYER, the eight labels (`0xA2C2C`) and the sixteen key names, then takes one key per slot, highlighting the current one. `svcmenu_register` adds `0x19DF0`. **Corrections (raw wins):** Esc cancels without applying (`0x1A3A4` jumps past the `0x1AE28` at `0x1A551`); Enter is not an exit but keeps the slot's current key; only completing slot 15 applies the record. A key is refused when an earlier slot holds its scan code (`& 0xFF00`) or when it has no name. There is no cursor movement: arrow keys are keys to bind. Two key codes the plan did not list: typing "spaten" into slots 0..5 sets the FREE PLAY flag `DS_00105D60`, and "morland" into slots 0..6 sets `DS_00108113` (read only at `0x4236F`). `0x19DD8` is dead code (no reference) and is not ported. `sm_check_keyboard` (25 scripted frames, K11 total 131 of 160) pins the slot map both ways, the out-of-range arms, the take-and-zero, a full run (both refusals, Enter, the "morland" flag, the applied record and the screen) and an Esc run ("spaten" sets FREE PLAY, nothing is applied). The duplicate refusal is tested against the slot just below (fix round 1), so the loop's upper bound is pinned but not its lower bound; the slot at which "morland" is tested and the stale-key drop at `0x1A331` are not tested either (each needs frames over the budget). Of 36 mutations, 34 fail the suite and those two (lower bound, "morland" slot) survive. The same fix round corrects §K11.5's mutation counts, which had counted the suite's closing `FAILURES` line. The counter is unchanged at 765/1203, because none of the four is a Ghidra function.

**all-gaps K11 cycle 6: STATISTICS pages 1 and 2 `0x328B8`..`0x33230` (record `2026-09-29-k11-service-menu-derivations.md` §K11.7).** `svcmenu.c` gains six functions. `0x328B8` draws m:ss at the text cursor (the minutes in `(w & 0xFFFF) - 3` cells padded with `' '`, the seconds in two padded with `'0'`). `0x32F54` is AVG TIME/COIN. `0x32F98` draws "Percentage Play" (`100 * (u16)(field 4 + field 5) / (fields 3 + 4 + 5)`) and "AVG TIME/COIN". `0x33458` draws the four average-time rows of the code-object table `0x326C4` (a field over one or two fields, as m:ss). `0x33058` is STATISTICS page 1 (the five rows of `0x32644`, four of them divided by 60, then `0x33458` and `0x32F98`). `0x33230` is page 2 "MORE STATISTICS" (the nine rows of `0x32674`). With its argument set (1 from `0x2CAC0`), holding Esc and Enter together zeroes config fields 0..0x27 once Esc is released. Page 1 draws once; page 2 draws once and again after a clear. Both leave on the latched Esc or Enter, or on a new pad Esc while Enter is not held. **Corrections (raw wins):** `0x2CA78` is `xor eax,eax; ret`, so `0x32F54` always returns 0 and its formula (as the plan reads it) is dead: the plan's "c = 7 gives 60" test cannot be run. `0x32F98` takes no mode (EBX and ECX are overwritten before use), and it draws AVG TIME/COIN at (col + 1, row + 1), a row above Percentage Play at (col, row + 2); its `je` at `0x33025` tests flags that are never zero. Page 1 reads no argument. `0x52106`'s argument is the last poll's key word (pinned), but `0x2F99C` runs `0x52106(0)` right after it, so it has no visible effect; page 2's alternating modes `0xF000`/`0x4000` select the same palette. One named gap (`PORT:`): a two-field sum with a zero low word makes `0x33458`'s `idiv` fault in the raw; the port draws 0. `sm_check_stats` (13 scripted frames, K11 total 144 of 160, leaving 16 for Task 8) pins the m:ss layout, both pages' draws with values that separate each `& 0xFFFF`, `/ 60` and field sum, all six exits, the no-redraw pass, and the clear (both keys needed, the wait for Esc's release, the bounds 0..0x27) through a new per-frame harness probe (`sm_probe`). Of 57 mutations, 54 fail the suite. Three survive and are listed: `0x32F54`'s zero test and `0x32F98`'s zero-total test (a division by zero gives 0 on AArch64, the same result), and page 2's mode alternation (invisible). The record also takes three Task 6 doc fixes. The counter is unchanged at 765/1203, because none of the six is a Ghidra function.

**all-gaps K11 cycle 7: the STATISTICS histograms and entry `0x2E218`..`0x2CAC0` (record `2026-09-29-k11-service-menu-derivations.md` §K11.8).** `svcmenu.c` gains seven functions. `0x2E218` counts the decimal digits of a signed value (1..10). `0x2E11C` zeroes one of the three audit histograms (`0x105ECD`/20, `0x105EE1`/20, `0x105EF5`/7 bytes, through the storage descriptors `0x2D45C..0x2D46C`) and sets its dirty bit 3..5 in `DS_00105DD8`; `config_storage_touch` (`0x2D4EC`, the declared no-op) is exported for it. `0x2E248` prepares a histogram from its code-object descriptor at `0x2D414` into the state block `0x105D64`: the title, the label column's width (bucket ranges such as " 20- 29", or the character names of the tabbed "Selects Per Character" template), the sum, the largest count and the median bucket. `0x2E5E4` formats one bucket line: the range or name, the count, the percentage and a bar scaled to the largest count, with "& UP" (or '+') on the last range. `0x32BDC` shows the three histograms one screen each (the median's line highlighted, MEDIAN and TOTAL below, "for next histogram"); the latched Esc or Enter or a new pad Esc goes on, and on the last one, when `a` is not 0 (with `a` = 0 the function returns 2 there), a new Esc with Enter held clears all three, then waits up to 89 frames for a new Esc. `0x33560` runs page 1, page 2 and the histograms, and `0x2CAC0` (OPTIONS MENU "STATISTICS", now registered) calls it with 1. **Corrections (raw wins):** the game's bar string is `"\x03"` (code `0x32640`), one cell, so the half cells `0x2E5E4` can draw never appear; the MEDIAN line is drawn only when the median's line has a ':', so histogram 2 shows none; the titles sit at the explicit column `(0x28 - len) >> 1`, not the centred -1; `0x2E5E4` bounds a bucket by the storage size, not the count; the tabbed template's result is the title length - 1; and `0x32BDC` returns its last EAX (a key word, 2, a poll word or -1), which the brief's `void` dropped (`menu_run` only tests -5 and -10, which no path gives). §K9.9 is revisited: `0x2E11C` is a second writer of the audit counters, zeros only and only from the options menu, which no oracle reaches. `sm_check_hist` (16 scripted frames, K11 total 160 of 160) pins the digit table, the clear, the state block and exact lines against a model written from the listings (with the code-object descriptors patched for the refusal, one-value, wide-bound and "& UP" arms), and four scripted runs through every exit, the clear and the hints. Of 60 mutations, 57 fail the suite and 2 crash it; one (Task 7's zero-total test removal, a division by zero that gives 0 on AArch64) survives. Six Task 7 review minors are folded in, among them a test of `0x32F98`'s zero-total value, and a re-measure that corrects §K11.4's mutation 42 from 44 to 43 checks. The counter is unchanged at 765/1203, because none of the seven is a Ghidra function.

**all-gaps K11 closed: the service menu (record `2026-09-29-k11-service-menu-derivations.md` §K11.0..§K11.9; ledger §B.1, §B.2, §E-28..33, §F-18).** Seven cycles ported 50 functions, 15 304 instruction bytes, in `port/src/game/svcmenu.c`: the 11 table callbacks (`0x2CB74`, `0x2CB94`, `0x2CACC`, `0x2CAC0`, `0x30EB4`, `0x30F54`, `0x31F24`, `0x19DF0`, `0x32358`, `0x30864`, `0x31138`), the 7 START MENU setters and every sub-menu they open (option editor, CONFIG OPTIONS, volume and handicap, MODIFY and TEST CONTROLS, CONFIGURE KEYBOARD, STATISTICS pages 1 to 3). Three of them are Ghidra functions (`0x2F464`, `0x319B0`, `0x31A78`), so the counter moved from 762 to 765 of 1203 (64%; 729 of 730 portable). The tests run through one seam, `host_set_pump_hook` (a `PORT:` hook in `host_pump`, NULL in the game), which lets `sm_*` script one key and pad word per presented frame; the scripted-frame budget is used in full, 160 of 160. Nothing in the service menu is reached by an oracle run (mode `0x27` needs a real Enter key), and the only `game_init` changes (two `0x2F9CC` stores and `svcmenu_register`) were proved frame-identical over 8000 frames, so every oracle line is unchanged. Named gaps (six): the language reload `0x47370` (a change is stored but not shown), the joystick device choice (stored, no host effect), the play-time fields (page 1 shows 0) and the audit counters (the histograms show 0); the TEST CONTROLS RAW DATA row that reads linear `0xFFE80003` (outside `mem[]`, drawn as 0); and `0x33458`'s `idiv` fault on a zero low word (drawn as 0); the CMOS save `0x1B084` stays deferred. Task 8 review minors are folded in (a can-not-fail `CHECK` became a comment, so the `CHECK` total is one lower; corrected header-grep, mutation-list and `a` = 0 wording; three untested arms and the `DS_00107448` result store recorded).

**K7 task 2: `0x1D0BC`, the ISR clock pair and the AIL end status (record `2026-09-29-k7-k12-derivations.md` §2).** `0x1D0BC` is ported as `sound_buffers_alloc()` in `flow.c`, and `game_init` calls it at the raw's point (`0x1C0B1`, after `0x1CF40`). It runs once (`DS_000A2CB0`). With a sequence and MDI handle it allocates the `0x5100`-byte MIDI buffer, which is dead in the port because `DS_001028C0`/`C4` stay 0. With a DIG driver and slot 0's buffer clear it gives the four sample slots `0x8C00`, `0x6000`, `0x6000` and `0x6000` bytes. If slot 0 ends with no buffer, it turns the DIG driver off, which is also what the raw does when that buffer was already set at entry. The allocator `0x1C308` stays host-owned, and its bump replacement is exported as `res_block_alloc`. The `1D0BC` row leaves `tools/port_classification.txt` (user decision §0.9.1). `game_isr_ticks(n)` models the timer ISR's counter pair (`0x1BE0E..0x1BE16`): the master loop's spin and the read stall now advance the `0x500BB` clock `DS_00101500` together with `DS_00101508`. `AIL_sample_status` now reports 2 once the mixer has no live voice for a playing handle, which is the port's form of the DIG service's end at `0x6F28F`. Named gap: with no device the mixer is not rendered, so a started sample stays 4 in `--check` and in the suite. Named gap, newly reachable (record §2.6 corrects §0.7.2, raw wins): the idle timeout `0x2EB80` is not confined to a key-wait loop. `menu_step` (`0x2FFC4`) calls it every master-loop frame (`0x303D9`), in the service menu (mode `0x27`) and the START MENU. Now that the spin advances the clock, `0x4B0` idle ticks (about 20 s) reach its store `DS_00107414 = 0` (`0x2EBA8`). The raw then longjmps (`0x2EBB3`, `0x65431` with `0x1044F4`), which the port does not model, so the port's menu re-initialises every `0x4B0` idle ticks instead. No oracle driver reaches it, and `check_idle_timeout_clock` pins the store over the ISR clock. Tests: `check_sound_buffers` has eight `0x1D0BC` vectors, with the peeked block sizes and the MIDI zeroing, plus the ISR pair. There is also the idle-timeout pin, the res stall's `DS_00101500` pin, `test_ail`'s status 2 and 4, and in `test_title_window` the `0x1C0B1` call and a clock that advances in every `game_loop` iteration. There are 45 new assertion sites (13299). All 17 measured mutations fail. Left untested: the two allocation-failure arms, which need an allocator that can fail. `make verify` stays green with every oracle line unchanged, the frame dumps and the `make audio-render` WAV are byte-identical, and the counter moves from 765 to 766 of 1203 (portable: 730 of 731).

**K7 task 3: `0x1CC28`'s slot choice, `0x1CB18`, `0x1CF20` and the retired announcer stand-in (record `2026-09-29-k7-k12-derivations.md` §3).** `snd_sample_queue` (`0x1CC28`) now queues on a slot, as the raw does:
- A sample above `0x6000` bytes goes only to slot 0.
- A smaller one takes the first free slot from 3 down. A free slot has a buffer, nothing queued, and a status other than 4.
- When no slot is free, the evicted slot is the last one, scanning from 3 down, whose queue time is strictly below now and below every slot scanned before it (unsigned). A tie keeps the first slot met, and the default is slot 0. The evicted slot is ended and re-inited.
- The queue writes `+0x04`, `+0x08` and `+0x14`.

`0x1CB18` is ported as `sound_sample_start(slot)`. It copies the sample into the slot's `0x1D0BC` buffer, sets the AIL sample up (the SFX volume, 11025 Hz, 8-bit, and loop count 0 only for loop byte 1) and starts it. `game_audio_service` (`0x1CF20`) calls it for slots 0..3 before the music. `PORT:` a bufferless slot is never started, where the raw would copy to linear 0.

The title announcer stand-in (voice `0xCD` at the title's first entry) is deleted, because the raw contradicts it (§0.7.5):
- The attract's phase 2 now plays its two looping s16title samples `0x40`/`0x42` (`0x11160`/`0x1116C`).
- The title's first entry stops them (`0x41`/`0x43` at `0x121CE`/`0x121D8`).
- The music request stays a `PORT:` stand-in (todo-verify §22).
- `main.c`'s `--check` probe now asserts that both loops play on the last attract frame and neither does two frames into the title.

Two of the brief's mutation expectations were wrong, and the raw won:
- `min >= t` survives the brief's "all at now" vector. The new tie vector E2 kills it.
- The buffer-guard mutation crashed the suite and lost its FAIL lines. Vector F now restores the low bytes it lets a mutant clobber.

The Task-2 review minor is closed: the C0 half of `0x1D0BC`'s MIDI gate has a vector.

Four assertions whose premise was the stub or the stand-in were rewritten, each with its raw address. There are 65 new assertion sites (13364), and all 22 measured mutations fail. Not tested: the exact `0x6000` boundary (no shipped payload has that size), the slot-0 arm's buffer test, and `0x1CB18`'s volume, rate and type values. The counter moves from 766 to 767 of 1203 (portable: 731 of 731).

**K12 batch A: the `0x100` stop-alls and the case-0/6 voices, plus the voice log (record `2026-09-29-k7-k12-derivations.md` §4).** The 22 batch-A calls to the voice dispatcher `0x2C3FC` are now made where the raw makes them, each with its `mov`/`call` pair. There are 19 `0x100` stop-alls in the attract tails and phase 0, the fight hooks `0x4367C`/`0x4454C`, the coin divert, player join, `0x25AE8`, states 4/5/6/7, mode `0x1E`'s three rank arms and the two menus. The other three are `0x53` (case 0) at the coin divert's tail, `0x29` (case 0) in `0x26D4C`, and `0xDE` (case 6) in the effects pass's type 7, which returns 0 and writes nothing. All are pure state, and none of them reads a bank. `sound_voice` gains a `PORT:` test seam that logs the ids it was entered with (`sound_voice_log_reset/count/at`). The shared runner `tf_voice_sites` drives one table row per wiring point from a public entry and requires the row's ids in the log in order, restoring the data object, the pools, the aperture and the DAC after each row. `test_voice_sites` holds batch A's 22 rows, and the later batches add their tables to it. Deleting any one of the 22 calls fails its own row and no other, except that rows 139/140 share one path and fail together. Correction (raw wins): `menu_run`'s `mov` is at `0x2FA66`, not the brief's `0x2FA68`. Task 3's review minors are closed:
- The `0x40` loop pin now renders past a one-shot's length (32 blocks, where 26 would already end one).
- Vector D pins the large arm's `+0x14` store, and vector E3 pins the scan's unsigned compare.
- The stale "announcer" comments are fixed.
- `check_sample_slots` restores the slot state it seeds.
- The eviction wording above is fixed.

There are 6 new assertion sites (13370). The `not wired` count drops by 18 lines (187 to 169). Rows 176/178/182 are split-phrase and keep their unwired `0xE1` half, and row 191's line keeps the unwired `0x22`, all for batch B2. `make verify` stays green with every oracle line unchanged, and the frame dumps and the `make audio-render` WAV are byte-identical. The counter is unchanged at 767 of 1203.

**K12 batch B1: the match-flow music requests and stops (record `2026-09-29-k7-k12-derivations.md` §5).** The 21 batch-B1 calls to the voice dispatcher `0x2C3FC` in `flow.c` are now made where the raw makes them, each with its `mov`/`call` pair. All are case 1 (music requests) or case 5 (stops): pure state, no bank read, no rng. They are `0x33` in `0x41578` and mode `0x12`'s state 5, `0x2E` in the hook `0x28D80`, `0x28`/`0x25` in the fighter hooks `0x26998`/`0x270BC`, `0x24` on three arms of the match-result text `0x28130`, the `0x27`/`0x22` pairs in the arena KO check `0x272DC`, the continue countdown `0x27A2C` and the round-over check `0x29970` (both sides), `0x2A` on the final arms of modes `0xD` and `0x32`, `0x25 + (n != 0)` on their replace arms (the raw's `setne` on the `DS_00104B0A ^ 1` flags), and `0x2B` at mode `0x33`'s expiry. Two corrections (raw wins, record §5.1). `0x29970`'s second test reads slot 1's `+0x5A` score byte `0x10789E` (`0x299AD`), not the `+0x7A` character byte `DS_001078BE` the port read, so side 1's round-over arm could never fire; `check_flow_round_over_check` now seeds the raw's byte, and restoring the old read fails it (3 FAIL lines). Row 143's gate is now ported: `0x28130`'s side-0/1 arm posts `0x24` only when the signed byte `DS_001088F2` is below 1 or that side's slot `+0x63` is non-zero (`0x28164..0x28178`, `0x281DA..0x281EE`). `test_voice_sites` gains `k12_b1[]` (21 rows), a six-case check of that gate and a check of the replace arms' other `setne` outcome. Before the wiring the suite printed 21 `voice site row` lines; after it, `all checks passed`. Deleting each of the 21 calls fails its own row (or its pair), and the gate, id and hard-coded-id mutations all fail. There are 4 new assertion sites (13376). The `not wired` count drops by 15 lines (169 to 154). `make verify` stays green with every oracle line unchanged, and the frame dumps and the `make audio-render` WAV are byte-identical. The counter is unchanged at 767 of 1203.

**K12 batch B2: the remaining pure-state voices (record `2026-09-29-k7-k12-derivations.md` §6).** The 30 batch-B2 calls to `0x2C3FC` are now made where the raw makes them, each with its `mov`/`call` pair. All are case 1 (song words) or case 5 (stops): the type-0x2D update's `0xF1` and type 0x09's tail `jmp` `0x4F` (`actors.c`), the attract's phase-4 `0x54`/`0x56` (`attract.c`, the one oracle-path point), the character-screen `0x30`s, the `0x2E`s of `0x4367C`/`0x4454C`, the versus hook's `0x31`/`0x2D`/`0x2F` and `0x4142C`'s `0x32` (`fight.c`), `0x3531C`'s `0xEC`/`0xE0` (`fighter.c`), and in `flow.c` the challenge poll's `0x2D`, mode `0x13`'s `0x2C`, `0x25AE8`'s `0x3D`, mode `0xF`'s `0x2B`, mode `0x1E`'s `0xE1`/`0xE3`/`0xE2` (states 0/4/5/7/8/`0xB..0x10`; one port pair for four raw pairs in states `0xB..0xE`), mode `0x1F`'s `0x3B` and `0x26D4C`'s `0x22`. `test_voice_sites` gains `k12_b2_game` (18 rows), and the new `test_fight_voice_sites` (`test_fight.c`, registered after it) holds `k12_b2_fight` (12 rows); Tasks 7-10 add their fight-area tables there (§6.3). Deleting any one of the 30 calls fails its own row (and the rows that share its path), and swapping row 13's `0x54`/`0x56` selection fails it too. Corrections (raw wins): row 25's `mov` is at `0x43272`, not `0x43285`; two of the `0xE2` calls are `0x1F113`/`0x1F029`, not `0x1F10E`/`0x1F024`; `0x2C3FC` never reads EDX (`0x2C3FF mov edx,eax`). Assertion sites 13372 -> 13374. The `not wired` count drops by 25 lines (169 to 144). The counter is unchanged at 767 of 1203.

**K12 batch C: the sample voices on the oracle paths (record `2026-09-29-k7-k12-derivations.md` §7).** This batch wires 22 calls to `0x2C3FC`, each at the raw's position and with its raw addresses. They are the first K12 voices that queue and start real samples on the enforced oracle paths.
- `attract.c`: the attract scheduler's `0xBD`, and `0xBE`/`0xBF` chosen by the `rng_next(2)` pick, which is now used rather than discarded.
- `actors.c`: animation opcode `0x2E`, whose id is the operand's low word.
- `fight.c`: the effects pass's crowd voices. `0xCD`/`0xCE`/`0xCF` are chosen by the `rng_next(3)` draw, and `0xC9`+`0xDA` or `0xCA`+`0xDB` by the second `rng_next(2)`. The trample's `0xD1`/`0xD0` is also here.
- `fighter.c`: fifteen fighter voices. `0x6E`, `0x6D`, `0x91`, `0xB1`, `0xB0`, `0xB3`, `0x6C` and `0xAF` are fixed. The rest are table words: `0xBDFFA`/`0xBDAA8`/`0xBE008` by char, and `0xE9308`/`0xE933C`/`0xE9358` by byte 7/8/9 of the reaction triple. `0xE933C[8]` is the case-3 `0x46`.

Tests: `test_voice_sites` gains `k12_c_game` (rows 6/7, with seeds that pick `0xBE` and `0xBF`). `test_fight_voice_sites` gains `k12_c_fight` (20 rows), whose drivers reach each site from a public entry. Every one of the 24 calls was deleted in turn, and each deletion fails its own row and any row that shares its path. The 11 selection and index mutations (the pick, the draw mapping, the second draw, the trample compare, the triple byte offsets and the char tables) fail too.

One correction, where the raw wins: the trample's id comes from the record's `+0x48` byte (`0x4B47B..0x4B489`, `sub 0x20; cmp 3; jge`, signed), not from "si < 3".

The gate result:
- `make verify` is green, and every oracle line is unchanged.
- The four frame dumps (`--check 8000`, the fe det runs, the attract and title dumps) are byte-identical to a before-dump and to the base manifest. So the samples now start on those paths, and no frame moves.
- The `make audio-render` WAV is byte-identical.
- Assertion sites go from 13371 to 13373.
- The `not wired` count in the `.c` files drops from 144 to 119. It is also gone from `attract.h` and `fighter.h`.
- The counter is unchanged at 767 of 1203.

**K12 batch D2: fighter.c's real-play sample voices, part 1 (record `2026-09-29-k7-k12-derivations.md` §9).** The 28 batch-D2 calls to `0x2C3FC` in `fighter.c` are now made where the raw makes them, each with its `mov`/`call` pair (or, for a table id, the index load through the call). - character 1's `0xB4`/`0x7C`/`0x7C` (`0x2365C`, `0x23130`, `0x23178`);
- the six `0xA8628` entrances' voices and their stream targets (`0xB4`, `0x8C`, `0xA2`, `0x9C`, `0x80`, `0x86`; `0x7B`, `0xB1`, `0xB9`, `0xA8`);
- the holds and finishers `0xC75AA[char]`, `0x59`, `0x4B`;
- `0x3A2A0`'s `0xBE008[char]`;
- character 4's `0xAB` and `0x47`.

`0x44A64`'s copy `0x44AFF` runs through row 100's shared call. Its table row (219) is Task 11's. `test_fight_voice_sites` gains `k12_d2` (29 entries: row 63's five ids are two entries). Each driver spawns both sides into the private pool or runs one entrance against a spawned side. Deleting any one of the 28 calls fails its own row and only rows on its path. So does reading a table through the wrong slot, dropping the index, or swapping row 63's selection. A probe build logged none of the 28 calls in `--check 8000`, the front-end, attract or title runs, which confirms "real play only". Corrections (raw wins): row 95's index load starts at `0x3E2F3`, not `0x3E2F9`. Row 73's §1.3 gates do not gate the voice. `0x2C3FC` restores EDX but never reads it.
